+import { expect, it } from "vitest";
+import { handleAttentionCommand } from "./attention-command.js";
+it("renders help without opening any database", async () => {
+  const output: string[] = [];
+  expect(
+    await handleAttentionCommand(["--help"], { stdout: (s) => output.push(s) })
+  ).toBe(0);
+  expect(output.join()).toContain("No daemon");
+});
+it.each([
+  ["status"],
+  ["status", "--default-tenant", "--default-tenant"],
+  ["status", "--tenant", "other"],
+  ["answer"],
+])("rejects unsafe/incomplete arguments %j", async (...args) => {
+  expect(
+    await handleAttentionCommand(args as string[], { stderr: () => {} })
+  ).toBe(1);
+});
+
+it("routes explicit observation release with the inventory version", async () => {
+  const calls: unknown[] = [];

+    await handleAttentionCommand(
+      [
+        "release",
+        "--scope",
+        "scope",
+        "--kind",
+        "session",
+        "--subject",
+        "session",
+        "--expected-version",
+        "7",
+        "--json",
+      ],
+      {
+        stdout: () => {},
+        rpc: async (method, params) => {
+          calls.push([method, params]);
+          return { ok: true, result: { released: true } };

+import { readAttentionStatus } from "./attention-read-model.js";
+
+const HELP = `Usage: osstack attention status --db /absolute/state.db --task ID --repo /absolute/repo --default-tenant [--json] [--cursor TOKEN]
+Read-only snapshot. No daemon is started and no database is migrated.
+Live process state is unknown; this command does not establish background supervision.
+Opt-in daemon: OSSTACK_ATTENTION=1 osstack serve
+  attention watch --task ID --repo PATH --default-tenant
+  attention inbox|inventory|deliveries --scope ID [--after ID] [--json]
+  attention get --scope ID --item ID
+  attention answer --scope ID --item ID --action ID --revision N --content-hash HASH --text TEXT [--takeover --grant ID]
+  attention release --scope ID --kind session|run|loop --subject ID --expected-version N
+  attention abandon-receipt --scope ID --session ID --message ID --expected-version N --reason TEXT --evidence-sha256 HASH --acknowledge-unknown-effects
+  attention brief|cockpit --scope ID --actor NAME
+  attention posture --scope ID --actor NAME --posture attended|quiet|away
+  attention seen --scope ID --actor NAME --revisions JSON
+  attention foreground --scope ID --subjects JSON
+  attention handoff --scope ID --client ID --token TOKEN
+  attention turn-end --scope ID --client ID --token TOKEN --role foreground_owner
+  attention loop --scope ID --loop-id ID --state-path PATH
+  attention ci --scope ID --github-repo OWNER/REPO --pr N --head SHA --required JSON
+  attention claude-hook --binding-file PATH
+  attention contract|routing-eval|triage|context|lesson --input PATH [--json]
+See docs/guides/attention.md for planning flags and verified hook setup.
+No daemon is automatically started. Hooks are explicit calls; no offline watchdog is installed.`;
+export async function handleAttentionCommand(
+  args: string[],
+  io: {
+    stdout?: (s: string) => void;
+    stderr?: (s: string) => void;
+    rpc?: (
+      method: string,
+      params?: unknown,
+      timeoutMs?: number
+    ) => Promise<unknown>;
+  } = {}
+): Promise<number> {
+  const out = io.stdout ?? console.log;
+  const err = io.stderr ?? console.error;
+  if (args.includes("--help") || args[0] === "help") {
+    out(HELP);
+    return 0;
+  }
+  try {
+    if (args[0] === "claude-hook") {
+      if (!io.rpc) throw new Error("daemon_connection_required");
+      const { handleClaudeAttentionHook } = await import("./attention-hook.js");
+      return await handleClaudeAttentionHook(args, io.rpc, out);
+    }
+    if (
+      ["contract", "routing-eval", "triage", "context", "lesson"].includes(
+        args[0] ?? ""
+      )
+    ) {
+      const { handleAttentionPlanning } = await import(
+        "./attention-planning-command.js"
+      );
+      return await handleAttentionPlanning(args, out);
+    }
+    if (args[0] !== "status") return await handleLive(args, io);
+    const values = new Map<string, string>();
+    const flags = new Set<string>();
+    for (let i = 1; i < args.length; i++) {
+      const key = args[i]!;
+      if (values.has(key) || flags.has(key))
+        throw new Error(`duplicate_flag:${key}`);
+      if (["--json", "--default-tenant"].includes(key)) {
+        flags.add(key);
+        continue;
+      }
+      if (
+        ![
+          "--db",
+          "--task",
+          "--repo",
+          "--after-run",
+          "--after-session",
+          "--cursor",
+        ].includes(key)
+      )
+        throw new Error(`unknown_flag:${key}`);
+      const value = args[++i];
+      if (!value || value.startsWith("--") || value.includes("\0"))
+        throw new Error(`missing_value:${key}`);
+      values.set(key, value);
+    }
+    const required = (key: string) => {
+      const value = values.get(key);
+      if (!value) throw new Error(`required:${key}`);
+      return value;
+    };
+    if (!flags.has("--default-tenant"))
+      throw new Error("explicit_default_tenant_required");
+    const result = readAttentionStatus({
+      dbPath: required("--db"),
+      repoPath: required("--repo"),
+      taskId: required("--task"),
+      defaultTenant: true,
+      ...(values.has("--cursor") ? { cursor: values.get("--cursor")! } : {}),
+      ...(values.has("--after-run")
+        ? { afterRun: values.get("--after-run")! }
+        : {}),
+      ...(values.has("--after-session")
+        ? { afterSession: values.get("--after-session")! }
+        : {}),
+    });
+    out(
+      flags.has("--json")
+        ? JSON.stringify(result, null, 2)
+        : [
+            "单次状态快照 · 未后台接管",
+            `Task: ${JSON.stringify(result.scope.task_id)}`,
+            ...result.sessions.map(
+              (s) =>
+                `${JSON.stringify(s.session_id)}: ${JSON.stringify(
+                  s.state
+                )} · ${JSON.stringify(s.wait.reason)}${
+                  s.decision ? ` · ${JSON.stringify(s.decision.question)}` : ""
+                }`
+            ),
+            `Runs: ${result.runs.length}; omitted: ${result.completeness.omitted_binding_unknown}; truncated: ${result.completeness.truncated}`,
+            `Limitations: ${JSON.stringify(result.limitations)}`,
+          ].join("\n")
+    );
+    return 0;
+  } catch (error) {
+    err(
+      `osstack attention: ${
+        error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)
+      }`
+    );
+    return 1;
+  }
+}
+
+async function handleLive(
+  args: string[],
+  io: {
+    stdout?: (s: string) => void;
+    rpc?: (
+      method: string,
+      params?: unknown,
+      timeoutMs?: number
+    ) => Promise<unknown>;
+  }
+): Promise<number> {
+  const command = args[0]!;
+  const allowed = [
+    "watch",
+    "inventory",
+    "inbox",
+    "get",
+    "answer",
+    "brief",
+    "posture",
+    "seen",
+    "foreground",
+    "handoff",
+    "turn-end",
+    "deliveries",
+    "cockpit",
+    "abandon-receipt",
+    "release",
+    "loop",
+    "ci",
+  ];
+  if (!allowed.includes(command)) throw new Error("unknown_attention_command");
+  if (!io.rpc) throw new Error("daemon_connection_required");
+  const params: Record<string, unknown> = {};
+  let json = false;
+  const keys = new Set([
+    "task",
+    "repo",
+    "scope",
+    "after",
+    "item",
+    "action",
+    "revision",
+    "content-hash",
+    "text",
+    "actor",
+    "posture",
+    "revisions",
+    "subjects",
+    "client",
+    "token",
+    "role",
+    "state-path",
+    "loop-id",
+    "github-repo",
+    "pr",
+    "head",
+    "required",
+    "session",
+    "kind",
+    "subject",
+    "message",
+    "expected-version",
+    "reason",
+    "evidence-sha256",
+    "grant",
+  ]);
+  for (let i = 1; i < args.length; i++) {
+    const flag = args[i]!;
+    if (flag === "--json") {
+      json = true;
+      continue;
+    }
+    if (flag === "--default-tenant") {
+      params["default_tenant"] = true;
+      continue;
+    }
+    if (flag === "--acknowledge-unknown-effects") {
+      params["acknowledge_unknown_effects"] = true;
+      continue;
+    }
+    if (flag === "--takeover") {
+      params["takeover"] = true;
+      continue;
+    }
+    const key = flag.slice(2);
+    if (!flag.startsWith("--") || !keys.has(key))
+      throw new Error("unknown_flag:" + flag);
+    const value = args[++i];
+    if (!value || value.startsWith("--"))
+      throw new Error("missing_value:" + flag);
+    const mapped: Record<string, string> = {
+      task: "task_id",
+      session: "session_id",
+      subject: "subject_id",
+      message: "message_id",
+      "expected-version": "expected_version",
+      "evidence-sha256": "evidence_sha256",
+      repo: "repo_path",
+      scope: "scope_id",
+      item: "item_id",
+      action: "action_id",
+      client: "client_id",
+      "content-hash": "content_hash",
+      "state-path": "state_path",
+      "loop-id": "loop_id",
+      "github-repo": "github_repo",
+      grant: "takeover_grant_id",
+    };
+    const name = mapped[key] ?? key;
+    if (params[name] !== undefined) throw new Error("duplicate_flag:" + flag);
+    params[name] = ["revision", "pr", "expected-version"].includes(key)
+      ? Number(value)
+      : ["revisions", "subjects", "required"].includes(key)
+      ? JSON.parse(value)
+      : value;
+  }
+  if (command === "answer") params["takeover"] ??= false;
+  const call = async (method: string) => {
+    const response = (await io.rpc!(method, params, 120000)) as {
+      ok: boolean;
+      result?: unknown;
+      error?: { code?: string; message?: string };
+    };
+    if (!response.ok)
+      throw new Error(
+        response.error?.code ?? response.error?.message ?? "attention_failed"
+      );
+    return response.result;
+  };
+  const result =
+    command === "cockpit"
+      ? {
+          inventory: await call("attention.inventory"),
+          inbox: await call("attention.inbox"),
+          brief: await call("attention.brief"),
+          deliveries: await call("attention.deliveries"),
+        }
+      : await call("attention." + command);
+  (io.stdout ?? console.log)(
+    json
+      ? JSON.stringify(result, null, 2)
+      : `Attention · ${command}\n${JSON.stringify(
+          result,
+          null,
+          2
+        )}\nDelivered means transport receipt; applied means instruction receipt; neither means task completed.`
+  );
+  return 0;
+}

+import { expect, it } from "vitest";
+import { classifyClaudeAttentionHook } from "./attention-hook.js";
+const binding = {
+  harness_version: "2.1.291",
+  session_id: "s",
+  scope_id: "scope",
+  client_id: "c",
+  token: "a".repeat(64),
+  role: "foreground_owner" as const,
+};
+it("accepts observed Claude root Stop shape and ignores cancellation, worker and repeated-stop shapes", () => {
+  const normal = {
+    hook_event_name: "Stop",
+    session_id: "s",
+    stop_hook_active: false,
+  };
+  expect(classifyClaudeAttentionHook(normal, binding)).toBe("remind");
+  for (const event of [
+    { ...normal, session_id: "other" },
+    { ...normal, stop_hook_active: true },
+    { ...normal, agent_id: "worker" },
+    { ...normal, hook_event_name: "SessionEnd", reason: "cancelled" },
+  ])
+    expect(classifyClaudeAttentionHook(event, binding)).toBe("ignore");
+  expect(
+    classifyClaudeAttentionHook(normal, {
+      ...binding,
+      harness_version: "unknown",
+    })
+  ).toBe("ignore");
+});
diff --git a/packages/cli/src/attention-hook.ts b/packages/cli/src/attention-hook.ts

+import { readFile, lstat } from "node:fs/promises";
+export interface ClaudeAttentionBinding {
+  harness_version: string;
+  session_id: string;
+  scope_id: string;
+  client_id: string;
+  token: string;
+  role: "foreground_owner";
+}
+/** Only a verified root Stop event can request a one-shot reminder. Never block Stop. */
+export function classifyClaudeAttentionHook(
+  event: unknown,
+  binding: ClaudeAttentionBinding
+): "remind" | "ignore" {
+  if (
+    binding.harness_version !== "2.1.291" ||
+    binding.role !== "foreground_owner"
+  )
+    return "ignore";
+  if (!event || typeof event !== "object") return "ignore";
+  const p = event as Record<string, unknown>;
+  if (
+    p["session_id"] !== binding.session_id ||
+    p["hook_event_name"] !== "Stop" ||
+    p["stop_hook_active"] !== false ||
+    p["agent_id"] !== undefined ||
+    p["agent_type"] !== undefined
+  )
+    return "ignore";
+  return "remind";
+}
+export async function handleClaudeAttentionHook(
+  args: string[],
+  rpc: (method: string, params?: unknown) => Promise<unknown>,
+  out: (s: string) => void,
+  input?: string
+): Promise<number> {
+  if (args.length !== 3 || args[1] !== "--binding-file")
+    throw new Error("binding_file_required");
+  const path = args[2]!;
+  const st = await lstat(path);
+  if (
+    !st.isFile() ||
+    st.isSymbolicLink() ||
+    st.nlink !== 1 ||
+    st.size > 4096 ||
+    (st.mode & 0o077) !== 0
+  )
+    throw new Error("private_binding_file_required");
+  const binding = JSON.parse(
+    await readFile(path, "utf8")
+  ) as ClaudeAttentionBinding;
+  if (typeof binding.token !== "string" || binding.token.length !== 64)
+    throw new Error("invalid_binding");
+  let raw = input ?? "";
+  if (input === undefined)
+    for await (const chunk of process.stdin) {
+      raw += String(chunk);
+      if (Buffer.byteLength(raw) > 65536)
+        throw new Error("oversized_hook_input");
+    }
+  if (classifyClaudeAttentionHook(JSON.parse(raw), binding) === "ignore") {
+    out("{}");
+    return 0;
+  }
+  const response = (await rpc("attention.turn-end", {
+    scope_id: binding.scope_id,
+    client_id: binding.client_id,
+    token: binding.token,
+    role: binding.role,
+  })) as { ok: boolean; result?: { reminder?: string | null } };
+  out(
+    JSON.stringify(
+      response.ok && response.result?.reminder
+        ? { systemMessage: response.result.reminder }
+        : {}
+    )
+  );
+  return 0;
+}

+import { mkdtemp, rm, readdir, readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
+import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
+import { join } from "node:path";
+import { expect, it } from "vitest";
+import { localAttentionSender } from "./attention-local-delivery.js";
+it("publishes one atomic local receipt across duplicate and concurrent delivery attempts", async () => {
+  const root = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), "attention-delivery-"));
+  try {
+    const sender = localAttentionSender(root);
+    const msg = {
+      delivery_id: "item:1",
+      scope_id: "scope",
+      item_id: "item",
+      question: "Choose?",
+      state: "open",
+    };
+    await Promise.all([
+      sender(msg, new AbortController().signal),
+      sender(msg, new AbortController().signal),
+    ]);
+    const files = await readdir(join(root, "attention-notifications"));
+    expect(files).toHaveLength(1);
+    expect(
+      JSON.parse(
+        await readFile(join(root, "attention-notifications", files[0]!), "utf8")
+      ).delivery_id
+    ).toBe("item:1");
+  } finally {
+    await rm(root, { recursive: true, force: true });
+  }
+});

+import { createHash, randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
+import { mkdir, writeFile, link, unlink, readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
+import { join } from "node:path";
+import type { AttentionSender } from "@osstack/core";
+/** Atomic local receipt keyed by delivery ID. Retries cannot duplicate the artifact. */
+export function localAttentionSender(home: string): AttentionSender {
+  return async (message, signal) => {
+    if (signal.aborted) throw new Error("aborted");
+    const dir = join(home, "attention-notifications");
+    await mkdir(dir, { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 });
+    const id = createHash("sha256").update(message.delivery_id).digest("hex");
+    const target = join(dir, `${id}.json`);
+    const temp = join(dir, `.${id}.${randomUUID()}.tmp`);
+    const body = JSON.stringify({ ...message, transport: "local_spool" });
+    await writeFile(temp, body, { flag: "wx", mode: 0o600 });
+    try {
+      if (signal.aborted) throw new Error("aborted");
+      try {
+        await link(temp, target);
+      } catch (e) {
+        if ((e as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code !== "EEXIST") throw e;
+        if ((await readFile(target, "utf8")) !== body)
+          throw new Error("delivery_collision");
+      }
+    } finally {
+      await unlink(temp).catch(() => {});
+    }
+  };
+}

+import { mkdtemp, writeFile, rm } from "node:fs/promises";
+import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
+import { join } from "node:path";
+import { expect, it } from "vitest";
+import { readAttentionLoop } from "./attention-loop-source.js";
+it("binds exact loop identity and never treats in-progress snapshots as live owners", async () => {
+  const root = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), "loop-source-"));
+  try {
+    const path = join(root, "state.json");
+    await writeFile(
+      path,
+      JSON.stringify({
+        loop_id: "l",
+        started_at: new Date().toISOString(),
+        updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
+        completed_tasks: [],
+        pending: [],
+        in_progress: [],
+        decisions: [],
+      })
+    );
+    expect(
+      await readAttentionLoop({ repo: root, path, loop_id: "l" })
+    ).toMatchObject({ process_liveness: "unknown", coverage: "snapshot_only" });
+    await expect(
+      readAttentionLoop({ repo: root, path, loop_id: "other" })
+    ).rejects.toThrow("identity");
+    expect(
+      await readAttentionLoop({
+        repo: root,
+        path: join(root, "missing"),
+        loop_id: "l",
+      })
+    ).toMatchObject({ state: "source_missing" });
+  } finally {
+    await rm(root, { recursive: true, force: true });
+  }
+});

+import { open, realpath, lstat } from "node:fs/promises";
+import { isAbsolute, relative } from "node:path";
+import { LoopState } from "@osstack/loop";
+/** No replay or cleanup port. A Loop JSON file is a lossy snapshot source. */
+export async function readAttentionLoop(input: {
+  repo: string;
+  path: string;
+  loop_id: string;
+}) {
+  if (!isAbsolute(input.path) || !isAbsolute(input.repo) || !input.loop_id)
+    throw new Error("explicit_loop_binding_required");
+  const repo = await realpath(input.repo);
+  let handle: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof open>> | undefined;
+  try {
+    const path = await realpath(input.path);
+    const rel = relative(repo, path);
+    if (rel.startsWith("..") || isAbsolute(rel))
+      throw new Error("loop_outside_scope");
+    if (!(await lstat(path)).isFile())
+      throw new Error("regular_loop_file_required");
+    handle = await open(path, "r");
+    const stat = await handle.stat();
+    if (!stat.isFile() || stat.size > 1024 * 1024)
+      throw new Error("unsupported_loop_source");
+    const buffer = Buffer.alloc(1024 * 1024 + 1);
+    const { bytesRead } = await handle.read(buffer, 0, buffer.length, 0);
+    if (bytesRead > 1024 * 1024) throw new Error("oversized_loop_source");
+    const state = LoopState.parse(
+      JSON.parse(buffer.subarray(0, bytesRead).toString("utf8"))
+    );
+    if (state.loop_id !== input.loop_id)
+      throw new Error("loop_identity_conflict");
+    return {
+      loop_id: input.loop_id,
+      path,
+      state: "snapshot_available",
+      updated_at: state.updated_at,
+      in_progress: state.in_progress ?? [],
+      pending: state.pending,
+      completed_count: state.completed_tasks.length,
+      wait: state.wait_reason ?? null,
+      process_liveness: "unknown",
+      coverage: "snapshot_only",
+      gap: "best_effort_snapshot_no_event_replay",
+      recovery_required: (state.in_progress ?? []).length > 0,
+    };
+  } catch (error) {
+    if ((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code === "ENOENT")
+      return {
+        loop_id: input.loop_id,
+        path: input.path,
+        state: "source_missing",
+        process_liveness: "unknown",
+        coverage: "uncovered",
+        gap: "source_missing",
+      };
+    throw error;
+  } finally {
+    await handle?.close();
+  }
+}

index 00000000..25d4ed36

+++ b/packages/cli/src/attention-planning-command.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,85 @@
+import { open, writeFile, mkdir, realpath } from "node:fs/promises";
+import { join } from "node:path";
+import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
+export async function handleAttentionPlanning(
+  args: string[],
+  out: (s: string) => void
+): Promise<number> {
+  const flags = new Map<string, string>();
+  for (let i = 1; i < args.length; i++) {
+    if (args[i] === "--json") continue;
+    const key = args[i]!;
+    const value = args[++i];
+    if (
+      !["--input", "--head", "--evidence", "--budget", "--repo"].includes(
+        key
+      ) ||
+      !value ||
+      value.startsWith("--") ||
+      flags.has(key)
+    )
+      throw new Error("invalid_planning_arguments");
+    flags.set(key, value);
+  }
+  const read = async (path: string) => {
+    const file = await open(path, "r");
+    try {
+      const data = Buffer.alloc(1048577);
+      const result = await file.read(data, 0, data.length, 0);
+      if (result.bytesRead > 1048576) throw new Error("oversized_input");
+      return JSON.parse(data.subarray(0, result.bytesRead).toString());
+    } finally {
+      await file.close();
+    }
+  };
+  const input = flags.get("--input");
+  if (!input) throw new Error("input_required");
+  const data = await read(input);
+  const planning = await import("@osstack/supervisor");
+  let result: unknown;
+  switch (args[0]) {
+    case "contract":
+      result = flags.has("--evidence")
+        ? planning.verifyDeliveryContract(
+            data,
+            await read(flags.get("--evidence")!)
+          )
+        : flags.has("--head")
+        ? planning.promoteScout(data, flags.get("--head")!)
+        : planning.deliveryContract(data);
+      break;
+    case "routing-eval":
+      result = planning.evaluateAttentionRouting(data);
+      break;
+    case "triage":
+      result = planning.acceptAttentionTriage(data, 1, {
+        category: "source_uncertain",
+        explanation:
+          "Use deterministic source facts; model output unavailable or invalid.",
+      });
+      break;
+    case "context":
+      result = planning.budgetAttentionContext(
+        data,
+        Number(flags.get("--budget") ?? 8000),
+        Date.now()
+      );
+      break;
+    case "lesson": {
+      if (!flags.has("--repo")) throw new Error("repo_required");
+      const repo = await realpath(flags.get("--repo")!);
+      const { renderLessonDraft } = await import("@osstack/loop");
+      const content = renderLessonDraft(data);
+      const directory = join(repo, "loop-lessons", "drafts");
+      await mkdir(directory, { recursive: true });
+      const path = join(directory, `attention-${randomUUID()}.md`);
+      await writeFile(path, content, { flag: "wx" });
+      result = { path, state: "draft", promoted: false };
+      break;
+    }
+    default:
+      throw new Error("unknown_planning_command");
+  }
+  out(JSON.stringify(result, null, 2));
+  return 0;
+}

+import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
+import { existsSync, realpathSync, statSync } from "node:fs";
+import { dirname, isAbsolute, join } from "node:path";
+import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
+import Database from "better-sqlite3";
+import {
+  AttentionStatus,
+  SessionDecision,
+  AttentionSessionFact,
+  type AttentionWait,
+} from "@osstack/protocol";
+import {
+  assertDbAccessAllowed,
+  defaultOsstackHome,
+} from "./control-plane-guard.js";
+
+export interface AttentionReadInput {
+  dbPath: string;
+  taskId: string;
+  repoPath: string;
+  defaultTenant: true;
+  cursor?: string;
+  afterRun?: string;
+  afterSession?: string;
+}
+export function attentionHash(value: unknown): string {
+  const canonical = (v: unknown): string => {
+    if (Array.isArray(v)) return `[${v.map(canonical).join(",")}]`;
+    if (v !== null && typeof v === "object")
+      return `{${Object.entries(v)
+        .sort(([a], [b]) => (a < b ? -1 : a > b ? 1 : 0))
+        .map(([k, x]) => `${JSON.stringify(k)}:${canonical(x)}`)
+        .join(",")}}`;
+    return JSON.stringify(v);
+  };
+  return createHash("sha256").update(canonical(value)).digest("hex");
+}
+export function guardAttentionDatabase(path: string): string {
+  if (!isAbsolute(path)) throw new Error("absolute_db_required");
+  const dbPath = realpathSync(path);
+  if (!statSync(dbPath).isFile()) throw new Error("regular_database_required");
+  const checkoutRoot = dirname(realpathSync(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)));
+  const defaultPath = defaultOsstackHome();
+  const defaultHome = existsSync(defaultPath)
+    ? realpathSync(defaultPath)
+    : defaultPath;
+  for (let home = dirname(dbPath); ; home = dirname(home)) {
+    if (
+      home === dirname(dbPath) ||
+      home === defaultHome ||
+      existsSync(join(home, "control-plane-reserved.json"))
+    ) {
+      assertDbAccessAllowed({ checkoutRoot, osstackHome: home });
+    }
+    if (dirname(home) === home) break;
+  }
+  return dbPath;
+}
+interface Row {
+  session_id: string;
+  run_id: string;
+  state: string;
+  version: number;
+  repo_path: string;
+  updated_at: number;
+  turn_id: string | null;
+  turn_state: string | null;
+  decision_json: string | null;
+}
+/** Persistent facts only; accepted is not proof that a live process is still sending. */
+export function readStoredWait(db: Database.Database, row: Row): AttentionWait {
+  const pending = db
+    .prepare(
+      `SELECT message_id,state FROM session_messages WHERE session_id=? AND direction='inbound'
+    AND state IN ('accepted','failed') ORDER BY CASE state WHEN 'accepted' THEN 0 ELSE 1 END,created_at,message_id LIMIT 1`
+    )
+    .get(row.session_id) as { message_id: string; state: string } | undefined;
+  if (pending)
+    return {
+      reason:
+        pending.state === "accepted"
+          ? "accepted_in_flight_or_uncertain"
+          : "delivery_failed",
+      owner: "unknown",
+      grant_id: null,
+      message_id: pending.message_id,
+    };
+  const grant = db
+    .prepare(
+      `SELECT grant_id,expected_turn_id,expected_version,body_json FROM session_continuation_grants
+    WHERE session_id=? AND status='active' LIMIT 1`
+    )
+    .get(row.session_id) as
+    | {
+        grant_id: string;
+        expected_turn_id: string;
+        expected_version: number;
+        body_json: string;
+      }
+    | undefined;
+  if (grant) {
+    let expires: number;
+    try {
+      expires = Date.parse(JSON.parse(grant.body_json).expires_at);
+      if (!Number.isFinite(expires)) throw new Error();
+    } catch {
+      return {
+        reason: "grant_unreadable",
+        owner: "unknown",
+        grant_id: grant.grant_id,
+        message_id: null,
+      };
+    }
+    const stale =
+      expires <= Date.now() ||
+      grant.expected_turn_id !== row.turn_id ||
+      grant.expected_version !== row.version;
+    return {
+      reason:
+        expires <= Date.now()
+          ? "grant_expired_unswept"
+          : stale
+          ? "stale_grant"
+          : "grant_coverage_unknown",
+      owner: stale ? "human" : "unknown",
+      grant_id: grant.grant_id,
+      message_id: null,
+    };
+  }
+  if (
+    ![
+      "idle",
+      "awaiting_decision",
+      "running",
+      "completed",
+      "failed",
+      "cancelled",
+    ].includes(row.state)
+  )
+    return {
+      reason: "unknown_state",
+      owner: "unknown",
+      grant_id: null,
+      message_id: null,
+    };
+  return {
+    reason:
+      row.state === "awaiting_decision"
+        ? "owner_decision"
+        : row.state === "idle"
+        ? "awaiting_instruction"
+        : "none",
+    owner: ["idle", "awaiting_decision"].includes(row.state) ? "human" : "none",
+    grant_id: null,
+    message_id: null,
+  };
+}
+
+export function readAttentionStatus(
+  input: AttentionReadInput
+): AttentionStatus {
+  if (
+    !input.taskId ||
+    input.taskId.length > 512 ||
+    !input.defaultTenant ||
+    !isAbsolute(input.repoPath)
+  )
+    throw new Error("explicit_scope_required");
+  const repoPath = realpathSync(input.repoPath);
+  if (!statSync(repoPath).isDirectory())
+    throw new Error("repository_directory_required");
+  const dbPath = guardAttentionDatabase(input.dbPath);
+  const scopeId = attentionHash([dbPath, input.taskId, null, repoPath]);
+  let page = {
+    scope: scopeId,
+    run: input.afterRun ?? "",
+    session: input.afterSession ?? "",
+    runDone: false,
+    sessionDone: false,
+  };
+  if (input.cursor !== undefined) {
+    try {
+      if (
+        input.cursor.length > 4096 ||
+        input.afterRun !== undefined ||
+        input.afterSession !== undefined
+      )
+        throw new Error();
+      const c = JSON.parse(Buffer.from(input.cursor, "base64url").toString());
+      if (
+        c.scope !== scopeId ||
+        typeof c.run !== "string" ||
+        typeof c.session !== "string" ||
+        c.run.length > 512 ||
+        c.session.length > 512 ||
+        typeof c.runDone !== "boolean" ||
+        typeof c.sessionDone !== "boolean"
+      )
+        throw new Error();
+      page = c;
+    } catch {
+      throw new Error("invalid_cursor");
+    }
+  }
+  let db: Database.Database | undefined;
+  try {
+    db = new Database(dbPath, {
+      readonly: true,
+      fileMustExist: true,
+      timeout: 1000,
+    });
+    const source = db;
+    return source.transaction(() => {
+      const schema = source.pragma("user_version", { simple: true });
+      // Main migrations 25–39 and Attention 40–41 preserve the v24 read projection.
+      if (!Number.isInteger(schema) || Number(schema) < 24 || Number(schema) > 41)
+        throw new Error("unsupported_schema");
+      const task = source
+        .prepare(
+          "SELECT task_id FROM tasks WHERE task_id=? AND tenant_id IS NULL"
+        )
+        .get(input.taskId);
+      if (!task) throw new Error("task_not_found_in_scope");
+      const runs = page.runDone
+        ? []
+        : (source
+            .prepare(
+              `SELECT r.run_id,r.phase,r.outcome FROM runs r JOIN tasks t ON r.task_id=t.task_id
+        WHERE r.task_id=? AND t.tenant_id IS NULL AND r.tenant_id IS NULL AND r.run_id>? ORDER BY r.run_id LIMIT 101`
+            )
+            .all(input.taskId, page.run) as {
+            run_id: string;
+            phase: string;
+            outcome: string | null;
+          }[]);
+      const rows = page.sessionDone
+        ? []
+        : (source
+            .prepare(
+              `SELECT s.session_id,s.run_id,s.state,s.version,s.repo_path,s.updated_at,
+        st.turn_id,st.state AS turn_state,st.decision_json FROM agent_sessions s
+        JOIN tasks t ON s.task_id=t.task_id JOIN runs r ON s.run_id=r.run_id AND r.task_id=s.task_id
+        LEFT JOIN session_turns st ON st.session_id=s.session_id AND st.ordinal=(SELECT MAX(ordinal) FROM session_turns WHERE session_id=s.session_id)
+        WHERE s.task_id=? AND t.tenant_id IS NULL AND r.tenant_id IS NULL AND s.session_id>?
+        ORDER BY s.session_id LIMIT 101`
+            )
+            .all(input.taskId, page.session) as Row[]);
+      const limitations = [
+        "loop_unsupported_in_slice_0",
+        "live_process_state_unknown",
+        "latest_turn_not_complete_history",
+        "run_repository_unverified",
+      ];
+      const sessions: AttentionSessionFact[] = [];
+      let omitted = 0;
+      for (const row of rows.slice(0, 100)) {
+        let bound = false;
+        try {
+          bound =
+            isAbsolute(row.repo_path) &&
+            realpathSync(row.repo_path) === repoPath;
+        } catch {
+          /* disclose below */
+        }
+        if (!bound) {
+          omitted++;
+          continue;
+        }
+        let decision: AttentionSessionFact["decision"] = null;
+        if (row.decision_json !== null) {
+          try {
+            if (row.decision_json.length > 16384) throw new Error("oversized");
+            decision = SessionDecision.parse(JSON.parse(row.decision_json));
+          } catch {
+            limitations.push(`invalid_decision:${row.session_id}`);
+          }
+        }
+        const fact = AttentionSessionFact.safeParse({
+          session_id: row.session_id,
+          run_id: row.run_id,
+          state: row.state,
+          version: row.version,
+          turn_id: row.turn_id,
+          turn_state: row.turn_state,
+          decision,
+          decision_hash: decision ? attentionHash(decision) : null,
+          process_liveness: "unknown",
+          wait: readStoredWait(source, row),
+          updated_at: row.updated_at,
+        });
+        if (fact.success) sessions.push(fact.data);
+        else
+          limitations.push(`corrupt_session:${row.session_id.slice(0, 512)}`);
+      }
+      return AttentionStatus.parse({
+        schema_version: 1,
+        mode: "snapshot_only",
+        scope: {
+          scope_id: scopeId,
+          task_id: input.taskId,
+          tenant_id: null,
+          repo_path: repoPath,
+        },
+        sampled_at_ms: Date.now(),
+        coverage: { state: "not_monitored" },
+        runs: runs
+          .slice(0, 100)
+          .map((r) => ({ ...r, repo_binding: "unverified" })),
+        sessions,
+        completeness: {
+          latest_turn_only: true,
+          truncated: runs.length > 100 || rows.length > 100,
+          omitted_binding_unknown: omitted,
+          next_cursor:
+            runs.length > 100 || rows.length > 100
+              ? Buffer.from(
+                  JSON.stringify({
+                    scope: scopeId,
+                    run: runs[99]?.run_id ?? page.run,
+                    session: rows[99]?.session_id ?? page.session,
+                    runDone: runs.length <= 100,
+                    sessionDone: rows.length <= 100,
+                  })
+                ).toString("base64url")
+              : null,
+          next_after: {
+            run: runs.length > 100 ? runs[99]!.run_id : null,
+            session: rows.length > 100 ? rows[99]!.session_id : null,
+          },
+        },
+        limitations,
+      });
+    })();
+  } catch (error) {
+    if (error instanceof Database.SqliteError)
+      throw new Error(
+        `${
+          error.code.startsWith("SQLITE_BUSY") ||
+          error.code.startsWith("SQLITE_LOCKED")
+            ? "source_busy"
+            : "unsupported_source"
+        }: ${error.code}`
+      );
+    throw error;
+  } finally {
+    db?.close();
+  }
+}
diff --git a/packages/cli/src/bin.ts b/packages/cli/src/bin.ts
index 51140ad6..1b3a7e61 100644

@@ -223,6 +223,7 @@ function renderChineseMainHelp(): string {
   stats               查看 agent 与 loop 指标
 
 项目命令：
+  attention status    只读查看任务状态与最新决策（未后台接管）
   graph               图执行 validate/run/status/cancel/resume；explain 只读关系诊断

+import { spawn, type ChildProcess } from "node:child_process";
+import { once } from "node:events";
+import { existsSync, readFileSync, statSync, symlinkSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
+import { rm } from "node:fs/promises";
+import { dirname, join, resolve } from "node:path";
+import { fileURLToPath, pathToFileURL } from "node:url";
+import { ModuleKind, transpileModule } from "typescript";
+import { createScratchDirSync } from "@osstack/core";
+import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
+import { acquireDaemonLock, DaemonAlreadyRunningError } from "./daemon-lock.js";
+
+let fixture: string;
+let pidFile: string;
+let moduleUrl: string;
+const children = new Set<ChildProcess>();
+const releases: Array<() => void> = [];
+
+beforeEach(() => {
+  fixture = createScratchDirSync("tests", "daemon-lock-ownership");
+  pidFile = join(fixture, "daemon.pid");
+  const sourceDirectory = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
+  const compiled = transpileModule(readFileSync(join(sourceDirectory, "daemon-lock.ts"), "utf8"), {
+    compilerOptions: { module: ModuleKind.ESNext },
+  }).outputText;
+  const compiledPath = join(fixture, "daemon-lock.mjs");
+  writeFileSync(compiledPath, compiled);
+  symlinkSync(resolve(sourceDirectory, "../node_modules"), join(fixture, "node_modules"), "dir");
+  moduleUrl = pathToFileURL(compiledPath).href;
+});
+
+afterEach(async () => {
+  for (const child of children) {
+    if (child.exitCode === null && child.signalCode === null) child.kill("SIGKILL");
+  }
+  await Promise.all([...children].map(async (child) => {
+    if (child.exitCode === null && child.signalCode === null) await once(child, "exit");
+  }));
+  children.clear();
+  for (const release of releases.splice(0)) release();
+  await rm(fixture, { recursive: true, force: true });
+});
+
+function acquire(): () => void {
+  const release = acquireDaemonLock(pidFile);
+  releases.push(release);
+  return release;
+}
+
+function launch(code: string): { child: ChildProcess; nextLine: () => Promise<string> } {
+  const child = spawn(process.execPath, ["--input-type=module", "--eval", code], {
+    stdio: ["pipe", "pipe", "pipe"],
+  });
+  children.add(child);
+  const lines: string[] = [];
+  const waiters: Array<(line: string) => void> = [];
+  let pending = "";
+  let errors = "";
+  child.stderr?.on("data", (chunk: Buffer) => { errors += chunk.toString(); });
+  child.stdout?.on("data", (chunk: Buffer) => {
+    pending += chunk.toString();
+    while (pending.includes("\n")) {
+      const newline = pending.indexOf("\n");
+      const line = pending.slice(0, newline);
+      pending = pending.slice(newline + 1);
+      const waiter = waiters.shift();
+      if (waiter) waiter(line); else lines.push(line);
+    }
+  });
+  return {
+    child,
+    nextLine: async () => {
+      const queued = lines.shift();
+      if (queued !== undefined) return queued;
+      return await new Promise<string>((resolveLine, reject) => {
+        const timer = setTimeout(() => reject(new Error(`child did not report: ${errors}`)), 8_000);
+        waiters.push((line) => { clearTimeout(timer); resolveLine(line); });
+      });
+    },
+  };
+}
+
+function ownerCode(afterAcquire = ""): string {
+  return `
+    import { acquireDaemonLock } from ${JSON.stringify(moduleUrl)};
+    const release = acquireDaemonLock(${JSON.stringify(pidFile)});
+    ${afterAcquire}
+    console.log('owned');
+    process.stdin.resume();
+    process.stdin.on('data', () => { release(); process.exit(0); });
+  `;
+}
+
+describe("daemon kernel ownership admission", () => {
+  it.each(["daemon.pid", "daemon.pid.lock.db"])("rejects a dangling %s symlink without creating its target", (file) => {
+    const target = join(fixture, "must-not-create");
+    symlinkSync(target, join(fixture, file));
+    expect(() => acquire()).toThrow("daemon_lock_requires_regular_file");
+    expect(existsSync(target)).toBe(false);
+  });
+
+  it.each(["daemon.pid", "daemon.pid.lock.db"])("rejects a %s symlink without modifying existing evidence", (file) => {
+    const target = join(fixture, "preserved-evidence");
+    writeFileSync(target, "existing data");
+    symlinkSync(target, join(fixture, file));
+    expect(() => acquire()).toThrow("daemon_lock_requires_regular_file");
+    expect(readFileSync(target, "utf8")).toBe("existing data");
+  });
+
+  it("refuses a second daemon in the same process", () => {
+    acquire();
+    expect(() => acquireDaemonLock(pidFile)).toThrow(DaemonAlreadyRunningError);
+  });
+
+  it("keeps one lock inode across ownership transfers", () => {
+    const release = acquire();
+    const identity = statSync(`${pidFile}.lock.db`);
+    release();
+    acquire();
+    const next = statSync(`${pidFile}.lock.db`);
+    expect({ dev: next.dev, ino: next.ino }).toEqual({ dev: identity.dev, ino: identity.ino });
+  });
+
+  it("an old release callback cannot release a newer owner", () => {
+    const oldRelease = acquire();
+    oldRelease();
+    acquire();
+    oldRelease();
+    expect(() => acquireDaemonLock(pidFile)).toThrow(DaemonAlreadyRunningError);
+  });
+
+  it("a stale diagnostic PID cannot override live kernel ownership", () => {
+    acquire();
+    writeFileSync(pidFile, "0\n");
+    expect(() => acquireDaemonLock(pidFile)).toThrow(DaemonAlreadyRunningError);
+  });
+
+  it("admits exactly one of eight synchronized processes", async () => {
+    const contenders = Array.from({ length: 8 }, () => launch(`
+      import { acquireDaemonLock } from ${JSON.stringify(moduleUrl)};
+      console.log('ready');
+      process.stdin.once('data', () => {
+        try {
+          globalThis.releaseOwnership = acquireDaemonLock(${JSON.stringify(pidFile)});
+          console.log('owned');
+        } catch (error) {
+          console.log(error.name === 'DaemonAlreadyRunningError' ? 'blocked' : 'error:' + error.message);
+        }
+        setInterval(() => {}, 60_000);
+      });
+    `));
+    expect(await Promise.all(contenders.map((entry) => entry.nextLine()))).toEqual(Array(8).fill("ready"));
+    for (const entry of contenders) entry.child.stdin?.write("go\n");
+    const outcomes = await Promise.all(contenders.map((entry) => entry.nextLine()));
+    expect(outcomes.filter((outcome) => outcome === "owned")).toHaveLength(1);
+    expect(outcomes.filter((outcome) => outcome === "blocked")).toHaveLength(7);
+  }, 15_000);
+
+  it("reacquires after SIGKILL without deleting diagnostic or lock files", async () => {
+    const owner = launch(ownerCode());
+    expect(await owner.nextLine()).toBe("owned");
+    expect(() => acquireDaemonLock(pidFile)).toThrow(DaemonAlreadyRunningError);
+    const stopped = once(owner.child, "exit");
+    owner.child.kill("SIGKILL");
+    await stopped;
+    expect(() => acquire()).not.toThrow();
+  });
+
+  it("a surviving exec child does not retain a dead parent's admission lock", async () => {
+    const descendantMarker = join(fixture, "descendant.pid");
+    const owner = launch(ownerCode(`
+      const { spawn } = await import('node:child_process');
+      const { writeFileSync } = await import('node:fs');
+      const descendant = spawn(process.execPath, ['-e', 'setInterval(() => {}, 60000)'], {
+        stdio: 'ignore', detached: true,
+      });
+      writeFileSync(${JSON.stringify(descendantMarker)}, String(descendant.pid));
+      descendant.unref();
+    `));
+    let descendantPid: number | undefined;
+    try {
+      expect(await owner.nextLine()).toBe("owned");
+      descendantPid = Number(readFileSync(descendantMarker, "utf8"));
+      const stopped = once(owner.child, "exit");
+      owner.child.kill("SIGKILL");
+      await stopped;
+      expect(() => process.kill(descendantPid!, 0)).not.toThrow();
+      expect(() => acquire()).not.toThrow();
+    } finally {
+      if (descendantPid !== undefined) {
+        try { process.kill(descendantPid, "SIGKILL"); } catch { /* already exited */ }
+      }
+    }
+  });
+});

+/** No application schema or migration. Kernel locks are released on process exit.
+ * Supported local FS only; SQLite's POSIX VFS does not inherit locks across fork.
+ * Never unlink/replace the lock DB: doing so would create two independent inodes.
  */
 export function acquireDaemonLock(pidFile: string): () => void {
-  if (existsSync(pidFile)) {
-    const pid = Number.parseInt(readFileSync(pidFile, "utf8").trim(), 10);
-    // Own pid counts as "running" too: a second startDaemon() in the same
-    // process (without releasing the first lock) must also be refused.

+  mkdirSync(dirname(pidFile), { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 });
+  const path = join(
+    realpathSync(dirname(pidFile)),
+    `${basename(pidFile)}.lock.db`
+  );
+  const localTypes = new Set([
+    0x1a, 0x11, 0xef53, 0x58465342, 0x9123683e, 0x01021994, 0x794c7630,
+  ]);
+  if (
+    !["darwin", "linux"].includes(process.platform) ||
+    !localTypes.has(Number(statfsSync(dirname(path)).type) >>> 0)
+  ) {
+    throw new Error(
+      `daemon_lock_unsupported_filesystem:${statfsSync(
+        dirname(path)
+      ).type.toString(16)}:${dirname(path)}`
+    );
+  }
+  if (held.has(path)) throw new DaemonAlreadyRunningError(process.pid, pidFile);
+  for (const candidate of [path, pidFile]) {
+    try {
+      const st = lstatSync(candidate);
+      if (!st.isFile() || st.isSymbolicLink() || st.nlink !== 1)
+        throw new Error("daemon_lock_requires_regular_file");

+  try {
+    if (db.pragma("journal_mode = DELETE", { simple: true }) !== "delete")
+      throw new Error("daemon_lock_wrong_journal_mode");
+    db.exec("BEGIN EXCLUSIVE");
+    held.add(path);
+    const fd = openSync(
+      pidFile,
+      constants.O_WRONLY |
+        constants.O_CREAT |
+        constants.O_TRUNC |
+        constants.O_NOFOLLOW,
+      0o600
+    );
+    try {
+      writeFileSync(fd, `${process.pid}\n`);
+    } finally {
+      closeSync(fd);
+    }
+  } catch (error) {
+    held.delete(path);
+    db.close();
+    if (
+      error instanceof Database.SqliteError &&
+      ["SQLITE_BUSY", "SQLITE_LOCKED"].some((code) =>
+        error.code.startsWith(code)
+      )
+    ) {
+      throw new DaemonAlreadyRunningError(diagnosticPid(pidFile), pidFile);
+    }
+    throw error;
+  }
+  let released = false;
   return () => {
+    if (released) return;
+    released = true;
     try {
-      // Only remove the pidfile if it is still ours.
-      const pid = Number.parseInt(readFileSync(pidFile, "utf8").trim(), 10);
-      if (pid === process.pid) unlinkSync(pidFile);
-    } catch { /* already gone */ }
+      if (diagnosticPid(pidFile) === process.pid) unlinkSync(pidFile);
+    } catch {
+      /* diagnostics only */
+    }
+    // Closing rolls back the empty transaction and releases the OS lock.
+    try {
+      db.close();
+    } finally {
+      held.delete(path);
+    }
   };
 }

+import { execFileSync } from "node:child_process";
+import { mkdirSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";

+import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
+import { startDaemon, type DaemonHandles } from "./daemon.js";
+
+let fixture: string;
+let priorHome: string | undefined;
+let daemon: DaemonHandles | undefined;
+
+beforeEach(() => {
+  fixture = createScratchDirSync("tests", "loop-recovery-retain");
+  priorHome = process.env["OSSTACK_HOME"];
+  process.env["OSSTACK_HOME"] = join(fixture, "home");
+  mkdirSync(join(fixture, "home"));
+});

+afterEach(async () => {
+  await daemon?.close();
+  daemon = undefined;
+  if (priorHome === undefined) delete process.env["OSSTACK_HOME"];
+  else process.env["OSSTACK_HOME"] = priorHome;
+  await rm(fixture, { recursive: true, force: true });

+async function listLoops(socketPath: string): Promise<Array<{ status: string; stop_reason?: string; executed_task_count: number }>> {
+  return await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
+    const client = connect(socketPath);
+    const decoder = new FrameDecoder();
+    const timer = setTimeout(() => { client.destroy(); reject(new Error("loop.list timed out")); }, 5_000);
+    client.on("connect", () => client.write(encodeFrame({ id: 1, method: "loop.list" })));
+    client.on("error", (error) => { clearTimeout(timer); reject(error); });
+    client.on("data", (data) => {
+      const response = decoder.push(data)[0] as { ok: boolean; result: { loops: Array<{ status: string; stop_reason?: string; executed_task_count: number }> }; error?: { message: string } } | undefined;
+      if (!response) return;
+      clearTimeout(timer);
+      client.end();
+      if (!response.ok) reject(new Error(response.error?.message ?? "loop.list failed"));
+      else resolve(response.result.loops);
+    });
+  });
+}
+
+describe("daemon interrupted Loop recovery", () => {
+  it.each(["running", "stopping"])("retains %s worker evidence and requires recovery instead of replay", async (status) => {
+    const workspace = join(fixture, "repository");
+    const worktreeRoot = join(fixture, "worktrees");
+    const orphan = join(worktreeRoot, "interrupted-worker");
+    mkdirSync(workspace);
+    mkdirSync(worktreeRoot);
+    mkdirSync(join(workspace, "tasks"));
+    const git = (...args: string[]): string => execFileSync("git", ["-C", workspace, ...args], { encoding: "utf8", stdio: ["ignore", "pipe", "pipe"] });
+    git("init", "-q");
+    git("-c", "user.name=Fixture", "-c", "user.email=fixture@example.invalid", "-c", "core.hooksPath=/dev/null", "commit", "--allow-empty", "-qm", "fixture");
+    const branch = "osstack/loop/interrupted-worker";
+    git("-c", "core.hooksPath=/dev/null", "worktree", "add", "-b", branch, orphan);
+    const evidencePath = join(orphan, "uncommitted-result.txt");
+    writeFileSync(evidencePath, "unconfirmed worker result");
+    const replayMarker = join(fixture, "unexpected-replay.txt");
+    writeFileSync(join(workspace, "tasks", "task.json"), JSON.stringify({
+      task_id: "interrupted-task", capability: "code.test", prompt: "Do not replay after an unproven interruption",
+    }));
+    const runOptions = {
+      workspacePath: workspace, useWorktree: true, worktreeRoot, sources: ["task_queue"],
+      executeCommand: `${JSON.stringify(process.execPath)} -e ${JSON.stringify(`require('fs').writeFileSync(${JSON.stringify(replayMarker)}, 'replayed')`)}`,
+    };
+    writeFileSync(join(fixture, "home", "loop-sessions.json"), JSON.stringify({
+      version: 1,
+      sessions: [{
+        loopSessionId: "interrupted-loop", status, runOptions,
+        intervalMs: 60_000, stopWhenIdle: false, startedAt: 1, updatedAt: 2,
+        cycleCount: 2, discoveredTaskCount: 1, executedTaskCount: 0, skippedTaskCount: 0,
+      }],
+    }));
+
+    daemon = await startDaemon({ socketPath: join(fixture, "d.sock"), adapters: [] });
+    const loops = await listLoops(daemon.socketPath);
+    expect(loops).toHaveLength(1);
+    expect(loops[0]).toMatchObject({ status: "stopped", stop_reason: expect.stringContaining("recovery_required"), executed_task_count: 0 });
+    expect(readFileSync(evidencePath, "utf8")).toBe("unconfirmed worker result");
+    expect(git("branch", "--list", branch)).toContain(branch);
+    expect(() => readFileSync(replayMarker)).toThrow();
+    await daemon.close();
+    daemon = undefined;
+    const persisted = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(fixture, "home", "loop-sessions.json"), "utf8")) as { sessions: Array<{ stopReason?: string }> };
+    expect(persisted.sessions[0]?.stopReason).toContain("recovery_required");
+  }, 15_000);
+});

index 00000000..1bf490ad
--- /dev/null
+++ b/packages/cli/src/daemon-startup-ownership.test.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,71 @@
+import { mkdirSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
+import { rm } from "node:fs/promises";
+import { join } from "node:path";
+import { createScratchDirSync } from "@osstack/core";
+import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
+import { startDaemon, type DaemonHandles } from "./daemon.js";
+import { acquireDaemonLock } from "./daemon-lock.js";
+import { pidFilePath } from "./paths.js";
+
+const cleanupFault = vi.hoisted(() => ({ enabled: false }));
+vi.mock("./update-check.js", async (importOriginal) => {
+  const original = await importOriginal<typeof import("./update-check.js")>();
+  return {
+    ...original,
+    startUpdateChecker: (...args: Parameters<typeof original.startUpdateChecker>) => {
+      const stop = original.startUpdateChecker(...args);
+      return () => {
+        stop();
+        if (cleanupFault.enabled) throw new Error("injected update checker cleanup failure");
+      };
+    },
+  };
+});
+
+let fixture: string;
+let priorHome: string | undefined;
+let daemon: DaemonHandles | undefined;
+
+beforeEach(() => {
+  fixture = createScratchDirSync("tests", "daemon-startup-ownership");
+  priorHome = process.env["OSSTACK_HOME"];
+  process.env["OSSTACK_HOME"] = join(fixture, "home");
+  mkdirSync(join(fixture, "home"));
+  cleanupFault.enabled = false;
+});
+
+afterEach(async () => {
+  cleanupFault.enabled = false;
+  await daemon?.close();
+  daemon = undefined;
+  if (priorHome === undefined) delete process.env["OSSTACK_HOME"];
+  else process.env["OSSTACK_HOME"] = priorHome;
+  await rm(fixture, { recursive: true, force: true });
+});
+
+describe("daemon startup and cleanup preserve ownership lifecycle", () => {
+  it("releases ownership when an existing non-socket path prevents startup", async () => {
+    const blockedSocket = join(fixture, "occupied.sock");
+    writeFileSync(blockedSocket, "operator evidence");
+    await expect(startDaemon({ socketPath: blockedSocket, adapters: [] })).rejects.toThrow("Refusing to remove non-socket");
+    daemon = await startDaemon({ socketPath: join(fixture, "valid.sock"), adapters: [] });
+    expect(daemon.socketPath).toBe(join(fixture, "valid.sock"));
+  });
+
+  it("releases ownership after an async listen failure even when startup cleanup throws", async () => {
+    cleanupFault.enabled = true;
+    await expect(startDaemon({ socketPath: join(fixture, "missing", "d.sock"), adapters: [] })).rejects.toThrow();
+    cleanupFault.enabled = false;
+    daemon = await startDaemon({ socketPath: join(fixture, "valid.sock"), adapters: [] });
+    expect(daemon.socketPath).toBe(join(fixture, "valid.sock"));
+  });
+
+  it("completes shutdown and releases ownership even when a cleanup hook throws", async () => {
+    daemon = await startDaemon({ socketPath: join(fixture, "valid.sock"), adapters: [] });
+    cleanupFault.enabled = true;
+    await daemon.close().catch(() => undefined);
+    cleanupFault.enabled = false;
+    const release = acquireDaemonLock(pidFilePath());
+    release();
+  });
+});

+import { AttentionInbox } from "@osstack/protocol";
+import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
+import type Database from "better-sqlite3";
+import {
+  AttentionStore,
+  AttentionObserver,
+  AttentionExperience,
+  AttentionActions,
+  attentionDigest,
+  type AttentionSender,
+} from "@osstack/core";
+/** The daemon has already acquired its kernel process lock before construction. */
+export class SupervisionService {
+  readonly store: AttentionStore;
+  readonly observer: AttentionObserver;
+  readonly experience: AttentionExperience;
+  private delivering = false;
+  private deliveryDone: Promise<void> | undefined;
+  private ciReader: import("@osstack/github").AttentionCiReader | undefined;
+  private timer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | undefined;
+  constructor(
+    db: Database.Database,
+    path: string,
+    readonly sender?: AttentionSender
+  ) {
+    this.store = new AttentionStore(db, path);
+    this.observer = new AttentionObserver(
+      this.store,
+      this.store.claimOwner(randomUUID())
+    );
+    this.experience = new AttentionExperience(this.store, this.observer.owner);
+    this.experience.recoverDeliveries();
+    new AttentionActions(this.store, this.observer.owner).recover();
+  }
+  start(): void {
+    this.scan();
+    this.timer = setInterval(() => this.scan(), 30000);
+    this.timer.unref();
+  }
+  private scan(): void {
+    try {
+      this.observer.tick();
+      const loops = this.store.db
+        .prepare(
+          "SELECT scope_id,subject_id,payload_json FROM attention_subjects WHERE kind='loop' AND admitted=1"
+        )
+        .all() as {
+        scope_id: string;
+        subject_id: string;
+        payload_json: string;
+      }[];
+      for (const row of loops) {
+        const path = JSON.parse(row.payload_json).state_path;
+        void this.handle("attention.loop", {
+          scope_id: row.scope_id,
+          loop_id: row.subject_id,
+          state_path: path,
+        }).catch(() => {
+          /* previous sample expires */
+        });
+      }
+      for (const scope of this.store.listScopes())
+        this.experience.enqueue(scope.scope_id);
+      if (this.sender && !this.delivering) {
+        this.delivering = true;
+        this.deliveryDone = this.experience
+          .drain(this.sender)
+          .catch(() => {
+            /* delivery state retained */
+          })
+          .finally(() => {
+            this.delivering = false;
+          });
+      }
+    } catch {
+      /* coverage expires even if timer is alive */
+    }
+  }
+  async stop(): Promise<void> {
+    if (this.timer) clearInterval(this.timer);
+    this.timer = undefined;
+    await this.deliveryDone;
+  }
+  async handle(method: string, params: unknown): Promise<unknown> {
+    if (!params || typeof params !== "object")
+      throw new Error("explicit_scope_required");
+    const p = params as Record<string, unknown>;
+    const str = (key: string) => {
+      const value = p[key];
+      if (typeof value !== "string" || !value || value.length > 4096)
+        throw new Error(`invalid_${key}`);
+      return value;
+    };
+    if (method === "attention.watch") {
+      if (p["default_tenant"] !== true)
+        throw new Error("explicit_default_tenant_required");
+      const scope = this.store.registerScope(
+        this.observer.owner,
+        str("task_id"),
+        str("repo_path")
+      );
+      this.observer.tick();
+      return this.observer.inventory(scope.scope_id);
+    }
+    const scopeId = str("scope_id");
+    if (!this.store.getScope(scopeId)) throw new Error("not_found");
+    if (method === "attention.release") {
+      this.store.release(
+        this.observer.owner,
+        scopeId,
+        str("kind"),
+        str("subject_id"),
+        Number(p["expected_version"])
+      );
+      return {
+        released: true,
+        execution_affected: false,
+        coverage: "uncovered",
+      };
+    }
+    if (method === "attention.loop") {
+      const { readAttentionLoop } = await import("./attention-loop-source.js");
+      const path = str("state_path");
+      const id = str("loop_id");
+      const facts = await readAttentionLoop({
+        repo: this.store.getScope(scopeId)!.repo_path,
+        path,
+        loop_id: id,
+      });
+      this.store.write(this.observer.owner, () => {
+        const binding = attentionDigest([path, id]);
+        const old = this.store.db
+          .prepare(
+            "SELECT binding_hash,admitted,payload_json FROM attention_subjects WHERE scope_id=? AND kind='loop' AND subject_id=?"
+          )
+          .get(scopeId, id) as
+          | { binding_hash: string; admitted: number; payload_json: string }
+          | undefined;
+        if (old && old.binding_hash !== binding)
+          throw new Error("loop_binding_conflict");
+        const count = (
+          this.store.db
+            .prepare(
+              "SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM attention_subjects WHERE admitted=1"
+            )
+            .get() as { n: number }
+        ).n;
+        const released =
+          old && JSON.parse(old.payload_json).reason === "owner_released";
+        const admitted = released
+          ? 0
+          : old?.admitted === 1 || count < 100
+          ? 1
+          : 0;
+        this.store.db
+          .prepare(
+            `INSERT INTO attention_subjects(scope_id,kind,subject_id,binding_hash,source_version,admitted,discovered_at,sampled_at,generation,payload_json) VALUES(?,'loop',?,?,0,?,?,?,?,?) ON CONFLICT(scope_id,kind,subject_id) DO UPDATE SET admitted=excluded.admitted,payload_json=excluded.payload_json,sampled_at=excluded.sampled_at,generation=excluded.generation`
+          )
+          .run(
+            scopeId,
+            id,
+            binding,
+            admitted,
+            Date.now(),
+            Date.now(),
+            this.observer.owner.generation,
+            JSON.stringify({
+              ...facts,
+              state_path: path,
+              reason: released
+                ? "owner_released"
+                : !admitted
+                ? "capacity_exceeded"
+                : facts.state === "source_missing"
+                ? "source_missing"
+                : facts.recovery_required
+                ? "recovery_required"
+                : "snapshot_only",
+            })
+          );
+      });
+      return facts;
+    }
+    if (method === "attention.ci") {
+      const { AttentionCiReader } = await import("@osstack/github");
+      this.ciReader ??= new AttentionCiReader();
+      return await this.ciReader.read({
+        repo: str("github_repo"),
+        pr: p["pr"],
+        head: str("head"),
+        required: p["required"],
+      });
+    }
+    if (method === "attention.brief")
+      return this.experience.brief(scopeId, str("actor"));
+    if (method === "attention.posture") {
+      const posture = str("posture");
+      if (!["quiet", "away", "attended"].includes(posture))
+        throw new Error("invalid_posture");
+      this.experience.posture(
+        scopeId,
+        str("actor"),
+        posture as "quiet" | "away" | "attended"
+      );
+      return { posture };
+    }
+    if (method === "attention.seen") {
+      if (!Array.isArray(p["revisions"])) throw new Error("invalid_revisions");
+      this.experience.seen(scopeId, str("actor"), p["revisions"]);
+      return { marked: true };
+    }
+    if (method === "attention.foreground") {
+      if (!Array.isArray(p["subjects"])) throw new Error("invalid_subjects");
+      return this.experience.foreground(scopeId, p["subjects"]);
+    }
+    if (method === "attention.handoff")
+      return this.experience.handoff(scopeId, str("client_id"), str("token"));
+    if (method === "attention.turn-end")
+      return this.experience.turnEnd(
+        scopeId,
+        str("client_id"),
+        str("token"),
+        str("role")
+      );
+    if (method === "attention.deliveries")
+      return this.store.db
+        .prepare(
+          "SELECT * FROM attention_deliveries WHERE scope_id=? AND delivery_id>? ORDER BY delivery_id LIMIT 100"
+        )
+        .all(scopeId, typeof p["after"] === "string" ? p["after"] : "");
+    if (method === "attention.inventory")
+      return this.observer.inventory(
+        scopeId,
+        Date.now(),
+        typeof p["after"] === "string" ? p["after"] : ""
+      );
+    if (method === "attention.inbox") {
+      const items = this.store.listItems(
+        scopeId,
+        typeof p["after"] === "string" ? p["after"] : ""
+      );
+      const counts = this.store.db
+        .prepare(
+          "SELECT state,COUNT(*) total FROM attention_items WHERE scope_id=? GROUP BY state"
+        )
+        .all(scopeId);
+      return AttentionInbox.parse({
+        schema_version: 1,
+        execution_takeover: false,
+        scope_id: scopeId,
+        items,
+        counts,
+        next_after: items.length === 100 ? items.at(-1)!.item_id : null,
+        coverage: this.observer.inventory(scopeId).coverage,
+      });
+    }
+    if (method === "attention.get") {
+      const item = this.store.getItem(scopeId, str("item_id"));
+      if (!item) throw new Error("not_found");
+      return item;
+    }
+    throw new Error("unsupported_attention_method");
+  }
+}

+/** Pure attention facts: no DB, process, model, or execution capabilities. */
+export interface WaitInput {
+  state: string;
+  turnId: string | null;
+  version: number;
+  now: number;
+  grant?: {
+    id: string;
+    status: string;
+    turnId: string;
+    version: number;
+    expires: number;
+  };
+  attempt?: {
+    state: string;
+    sourceTurn: string;
+    sourceVersion: number;
+    reservationVersion?: number;
+    messageId?: string;
+  };
+  message?: { id: string; state: string };
+  /** Only a local, exact message reservation can establish this. */
+  applyingMessageId?: string;
+  scheduled?: boolean;
+}
+export function classifyAttentionWait(input: WaitInput): {
+  reason: string;
+  owner: "human" | "system" | "unknown" | "none";
+  blocked: boolean;
+} {
+  const { grant, attempt, message } = input;
+  const validGrant =
+    grant?.status === "active" &&
+    Number.isFinite(grant.expires) &&
+    grant.expires > input.now;
+  const matchingReservation =
+    validGrant &&
+    attempt?.state === "reserved" &&
+    attempt.sourceTurn === input.turnId &&
+    attempt.sourceVersion === grant.version &&
+    attempt.sourceTurn === grant.turnId &&
+    attempt.reservationVersion === input.version &&
+    attempt.messageId === message?.id &&
+    message?.state === "accepted";
+  if (message && ["accepted", "failed"].includes(message.state))
+    return {
+      reason:
+        message.state === "failed"
+          ? "delivery_failed"
+          : input.applyingMessageId === message.id
+          ? "in_flight"
+          : "accepted_in_flight_or_uncertain",
+      owner: matchingReservation ? "system" : "unknown",
+      blocked: true,
+    };
+  if (attempt && ["checking", "reserved"].includes(attempt.state)) {
+    const checking =
+      validGrant &&
+      attempt.state === "checking" &&
+      attempt.sourceTurn === input.turnId &&
+      attempt.sourceVersion === input.version &&
+      grant.turnId === input.turnId &&
+      grant.version === input.version;
+    return {
+      reason: checking
+        ? input.scheduled
+          ? "checking"
+          : "grant_coverage_unknown"
+        : "stale_attempt",
+      owner: checking && input.scheduled ? "system" : "unknown",
+      blocked: true,
+    };
+  }
+  if (grant?.status === "active") {
+    const current =
+      validGrant &&
+      grant.turnId === input.turnId &&
+      grant.version === input.version;
+    return {
+      reason: current ? "grant_coverage_unknown" : "stale_grant",
+      owner: current ? "unknown" : "human",
+      blocked: true,
+    };
+  }
+  if (input.state === "awaiting_decision")
+    return { reason: "owner_decision", owner: "human", blocked: false };
+  if (input.state === "idle")
+    return { reason: "awaiting_instruction", owner: "human", blocked: false };
+  return { reason: "none", owner: "none", blocked: false };
+}
+
+export function evaluateCoverage(input: {
+  now: number;
+  sampled: number;
+  deadline: number;
+  complete: boolean;
+  ownerCurrent: boolean;
+}): "covered" | "degraded" | "uncovered" | "unknown" {
+  if (
+    ![input.now, input.sampled, input.deadline].every(Number.isFinite) ||
+    input.now < input.sampled
+  )
+    return "unknown";
+  if (!input.ownerCurrent || input.now > input.deadline) return "uncovered";
+  return input.complete ? "covered" : "degraded";
+}
+export function evaluateActivity(input: {
+  kind: "run" | "session";
+  businessState: string;
+  instance: string;
+  now: number;
+  observation?: {
+    instance: string;
+    state: "busy" | "idle" | "dead";
+    expires: number;
+  };
+}): {
+  business_state: string;
+  process_liveness: "busy" | "idle" | "unknown" | "dead";
+} {
+  const observation = input.observation;
+  const valid =
+    observation &&
+    observation.instance === input.instance &&
+    Number.isFinite(observation.expires) &&
+    Number.isFinite(input.now) &&
+    input.now <= observation.expires &&
+    !(input.kind === "session" && observation.state === "dead");
+  return {
+    business_state: input.businessState,
+    process_liveness: valid ? observation.state : "unknown",
+  };
+}

+import { mkdtempSync, realpathSync, rmSync } from "node:fs";
+import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
+import { join } from "node:path";
+import { afterEach, expect, it } from "vitest";
+import { openDb } from "./db.js";
+import { AttentionStore } from "./attention-store.js";
+import {
+  AttentionActions,
+  reserveSessionInstruction,
+} from "./attention-actions.js";
+const cleanup: Array<() => void> = [];
+afterEach(() => cleanup.splice(0).forEach((f) => { f(); }));
+function setup() {
+  const root = realpathSync(mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "actions-")));
+  const path = join(root, "db");
+  const db = openDb({ path });
+  cleanup.push(() => {
+    db.close();
+    rmSync(root, { recursive: true, force: true });
+  });
+  db.exec(
+    `INSERT INTO tasks(task_id,title,status,created_at,updated_at) VALUES('t','t','running',1,1); INSERT INTO runs(run_id,task_id,session_id,agent_id,capability_id,phase,started_at) VALUES('r','t','s','codex-cli','code','execute',1)`
+  );
+  db.prepare(
+    `INSERT INTO agent_sessions(session_id,task_id,run_id,agent_id,request_sha256,repo_path,state,resume_supported,version,updated_at,native_session_id) VALUES('s','t','r','codex-cli',?,?,'awaiting_decision',1,1,1,'native')`
+  ).run("a".repeat(64), root);
+  const decision = { decision_id: "d", question: "Choose?", choices: [] };
+  db.prepare(
+    "INSERT INTO session_turns(turn_id,session_id,ordinal,state,decision_json,created_at,updated_at) VALUES('turn','s',1,'awaiting_decision',?,1,1)"
+  ).run(JSON.stringify(decision));
+  const store = new AttentionStore(db, path);
+  const owner = store.claimOwner("a");
+  const scope = store.registerScope(owner, "t", root);
+  const item = store.projectDecision(owner, {
+    scopeId: scope.scope_id,
+    sessionId: "s",
+    turnId: "turn",
+    sourceVersion: 1,
+    decision,
+    now: 1,
+  });
+  const input = {
+    scope_id: scope.scope_id,
+    item_id: item.item_id,
+    action_id: "a",
+    revision: item.revision,
+    content_hash: item.content_hash,
+    text: "A",
+    takeover: false,
+  };
+  return { db, path, input, actions: new AttentionActions(store, owner) };
+}
+it("reserves once, returns uncertainty on retry, and prevents replacement IDs after ambiguous delivery", () => {
+  const { actions, input, db } = setup();
+  const first = actions.reserve(input);
+  const second = actions.reserve(input);
+  expect(second.message_id).toBe(first.message_id);
+  expect(second.state).toBe("uncertain");
+  expect(() => actions.reserve({ ...input, action_id: "b" })).toThrow();
+  expect(() => actions.reserve({ ...input, text: "B" })).toThrow(
+    "action_id_conflict"
+  );
+  expect(
+    (
+      db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM session_messages").get() as {
+        n: number;
+      }
+    ).n
+  ).toBe(1);
+});
+it("reconciles an applied exact receipt without claiming task completion", () => {
+  const { actions, input, db } = setup();
+  const a = actions.reserve(input);
+  db.prepare(
+    "UPDATE session_messages SET state='applied' WHERE message_id=?"
+  ).run(a.message_id);
+  expect(actions.reserve(input).state).toBe("applied");
+  expect(
+    (
+      db.prepare("SELECT status FROM tasks WHERE task_id='t'").get() as {
+        status: string;
+      }
+    ).status
+  ).toBe("running");
+});
+it("serializes a direct send against an inbox reservation", () => {
+  const { actions, input, db, path } = setup();
+  actions.reserve(input);
+  expect(() =>
+    reserveSessionInstruction(
+      db,
+      path,
+      {
+        message_id: "other",
+        session_id: "s",
+        turn_id: "turn",
+        direction: "inbound",
+        kind: "instruction.send",
+        payload: { text: "B" },
+        state: "accepted",
+        created_at: 2,
+      },
+      2,
+      2
+    )
+  ).toThrow("delivery_uncertain");
+});
+it("requires a signed approval route for risk gates", () => {
+  const { actions, input, db } = setup();
+  db.exec(
+    `UPDATE session_turns SET decision_json='{"decision_id":"d","question":"Choose?","choices":[],"continuation":{"version":1,"gate_kind":"risk_approval","phase":"deploy","next_action":"deploy","evidence_refs":[]}}'`
+  );
+  expect(() => actions.reserve(input)).toThrow("source_changed");
+  expect(db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM attention_actions").get()).toBeUndefined();
+});
+it("refuses terminal session revival and recovers crash-window reservations", () => {
+  const { actions, input, db } = setup();
+  db.exec("UPDATE agent_sessions SET state='cancelled'");
+  expect(() => actions.reserve(input)).toThrow("session_not_awaiting_decision");
+  db.exec("UPDATE agent_sessions SET state='awaiting_decision'");
+  actions.reserve(input);
+  actions.recover();
+  expect(
+    (db.prepare("SELECT state FROM attention_items").get() as { state: string })
+      .state
+  ).toBe("answer_uncertain");
+});
+it("requires risk approval even when source digest is unchanged, and provides explicit abandonment without replay", () => {
+  const { actions, input, db, path } = setup();
+  db.prepare("UPDATE session_turns SET decision_json=?").run(
+    JSON.stringify({
+      decision_id: "d",
+      question: "Deploy?",
+      choices: [],
+      continuation: {
+        version: 1,
+        gate_kind: "risk_approval",
+        phase: "deploy",
+        next_action: "deploy",
+        evidence_refs: [],
+      },
+    })
+  );
+  expect(() =>
+    reserveSessionInstruction(
+      db,
+      path,
+      {
+        message_id: "risk",
+        session_id: "s",
+        turn_id: "turn",
+        direction: "inbound",
+        kind: "instruction.send",
+        payload: { text: "yes" },
+        state: "accepted",
+        created_at: 1,
+      },
+      1,
+      1
+    )
+  ).toThrow("signed_approval_required");
+  db.prepare("UPDATE session_turns SET decision_json=?").run(
+    JSON.stringify({ decision_id: "d", question: "Choose?", choices: [] })
+  );
+  const reserved = actions.reserve(input);
+  actions.recover();
+  expect(
+    actions.abandon({
+      scope_id: input.scope_id,
+      session_id: "s",
+      message_id: reserved.message_id,
+      expected_version: 2,
+      reason:
+        "Owner inspected uncertain effects; discard original instruction without replay",
+      evidence_sha256: "a".repeat(64),
+    })
+  ).toMatchObject({ disposition: "abandoned_unknown", replayed: false });
+  expect(actions.reserve(input).state).toBe("abandoned_unknown");
+  expect(
+    reserveSessionInstruction(
+      db,
+      path,
+      {
+        message_id: "new-manual",
+        session_id: "s",
+        turn_id: "turn",
+        direction: "inbound",
+        kind: "instruction.send",
+        payload: { text: "Inspect current state only" },
+        state: "accepted",
+        created_at: 3,
+      },
+      2,
+      3
+    ).session_version
+  ).toBe(3);
+});
+it("rejects malformed structured risk decisions on the direct manual path", () => {
+  const { db, path } = setup();
+  db.prepare("UPDATE session_turns SET decision_json=?").run(
+    JSON.stringify({
+      decision_id: "risk",
+      choices: [],
+      continuation: { gate_kind: "risk_approval" },
+    })
+  );
+  expect(() =>
+    reserveSessionInstruction(
+      db,
+      path,
+      {
+        message_id: "m",
+        session_id: "s",
+        turn_id: "turn",
+        direction: "inbound",
+        kind: "instruction.send",
+        payload: { text: "yes" },
+        state: "accepted",
+        created_at: 1,
+      },
+      1,
+      1
+    )
+  ).toThrow("invalid_decision");
+  expect(db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM session_messages").get()).toBeUndefined();
+});
+
+it("rejects an awaiting-decision turn with no decision on the direct path", () => {
+  const { db, path } = setup();
+  db.exec("UPDATE session_turns SET decision_json=NULL");
+  expect(() =>
+    reserveSessionInstruction(
+      db,
+      path,
+      {
+        message_id: "missing",
+        session_id: "s",
+        turn_id: "turn",
+        direction: "inbound",
+        kind: "instruction.send",
+        payload: { text: "yes" },
+        state: "accepted",
+        created_at: 1,
+      },
+      1,
+      1
+    )
+  ).toThrow("invalid_decision");
+  expect(db.prepare("SELECT 1 FROM session_messages").get()).toBeUndefined();
+});

+++ b/packages/core/src/state/attention-actions.ts
