    graphBroker ??= import("./graph-action-broker.js").then(({ GraphActionBroker }) => new GraphActionBroker(ctx.db, {
      acquire: (input) => admissionFor(ctx).acquire(input),
      authorizePolicy: async (workspacePath) => {
        const decision = await makeRepositoryPolicyGate(workspacePath, true)({ workspacePath });
        if (!decision.allow) throw new Error(decision.reason);
      },
    }));
    return graphBroker;
  };
  const registeredWorkspace = opts.workspacePath === undefined ? undefined : (await resolveRepositoryScope(opts.workspacePath)).workspacePath;
  const socketPath = opts.socketPath ?? resolveSocketPath();
  const continuationTrust = new Map(opts.continuationTrustedPublicKeys
    ?? await readContinuationTrust(join(osstackHome(), "continuation-trust.json")));
  // Refuse to start when another daemon already owns the pidfile — two
  // daemons would race for the socket and share the SQLite writer.
  const releaseLock = acquireDaemonLock(pidFilePath());
  let failedContext: DaemonContext | undefined;
  let stopUpdates: (() => void) | undefined;
  let failedServer: ReturnType<typeof createServer> | undefined;
  try {
  cleanupStaleSocket(socketPath);
  // Bring up OTel before the daemon context so spans capture everything.
  await initOtel({ serviceVersion: DAEMON_VERSION });
  const ctx = buildContext({
    socketPath,
    ...(opts.adapters === undefined ? {} : { adapters: opts.adapters }),
    ...(opts.writeConcurrency === undefined
      ? {}
      : { writeConcurrency: opts.writeConcurrency }),
    ...(opts.readConcurrency === undefined
      ? {}
      : { readConcurrency: opts.readConcurrency }),
  });
  failedContext = ctx;
  if (process.env["OSSTACK_ATTENTION"] === "1") { ctx.supervision = new SupervisionService(ctx.db, ctx.dbPath, process.env["OSSTACK_ATTENTION_NOTIFY"] === "local" ? localAttentionSender(osstackHome()) : undefined); ctx.supervision.start(); }
  ctx.collaborator = new SessionCollaborator({
    repository: ctx.continuations,
    adapters: ctx.adapters,
    trustedPublicKeys: continuationTrust,
    draining: () => collaboratorPaused(ctx),
    send: async (reservation, controller) => {
      const session = ctx.sessions.getSession(reservation.grant.body.session_id);
      const adapter = ctx.adapters.get(reservation.grant.body.runtime_id);
      if (collaboratorPaused(ctx) || controller.signal.aborted) return { ok: false, error: { code: "continuation_cancelled" } };
      if (session === undefined || adapter === undefined || !isSessionCapableAdapter(adapter)) {
        return { ok: false, error: { code: "resume_unsupported" } };
      }
      if (ctx.activeSessionSends.has(session.session_id)) return { ok: false, error: { code: "session_busy" } };
      return await executeReservedSessionTurn(ctx, { id: reservation.attempt.attempt_id, method: "collaborator.resume" }, {
        session_id: session.session_id,
        runtime_id: session.agent_id,
        turn_id: reservation.message.turn_id,
        message_id: reservation.message.message_id,
        expected_version: reservation.reservation_version - 1,
        text: reservation.prompt,
        requested_by: `agent:collaborator:${reservation.grant.body.grant_id}`,
      }, session, adapter, reservation.reservation_version, {
        controller,
        completed: (turnId, version, now) => {
          // Timers are cooperative: a blocked event loop can deliver completion
          // before an overdue abort callback. Keep the real receipt, but expire
          // the delegation inside this same completion transaction.
          const grant = reservation.grant;
          if (now * 1000 >= Date.parse(grant.body.expires_at)) ctx.continuations.block(grant.grant_id, "expired", now);
          else if ((now - reservation.message.created_at) * 1000 >= grant.body.turn_timeout_ms) ctx.continuations.block(grant.grant_id, "turn_timeout", now);
          ctx.continuations.completeAttempt(reservation.attempt.attempt_id, turnId, version, now);
        },
      });
    },
  });
  if (registeredWorkspace !== undefined || opts.adapterWorkspaces !== undefined) {
    ctx.adapterWorkspaces = new Map([
      ...(registeredWorkspace === undefined ? [] : [...ctx.adapters.keys()].map(id => [id, registeredWorkspace] as const)),
      ...(opts.adapterWorkspaces ?? []),
    ]);
  }
  if (opts.autonomyHealth !== undefined) {
    ctx.autonomyHealth = opts.autonomyHealth;
  }
  if (opts.guardedAutonomyFactory !== undefined) {
    ctx.guardedAutonomy = await opts.guardedAutonomyFactory(
      ctx.db,
      ctx.dbPath,
    );
  }
  const stopUpdateChecker = startUpdateChecker();
  stopUpdates = stopUpdateChecker;
  let dashboard: DashboardServerHandle | null = null;

  const server = createServer((sock: Socket) => {
    graphSockets.add(sock);
    sock.once('close', () => graphSockets.delete(sock));
    const decoder = new FrameDecoder();

    sock.on("data", (chunk) => {
      try {
        const frames = decoder.push(chunk);
        for (const raw of frames) {
          const req = raw as DaemonRequest;
          if (typeof req !== "object" || req === null || typeof req.method !== "string") {
            sock.write(encodeFrame(singleErr(req?.id ?? 0, "bad_request", "request must have a 'method' string")));
            continue;
          }

          // Wall-clock per request, recorded into the per-method latency
          // window that `osstack stats` reports. For the streaming methods
          // (task.submit/task.observe) this measures the full handler
          // lifetime, not time-to-first-byte.
          const startedAt = performance.now();
          const recordLatency = (): void => {
            ctx.rpcLatency.record(req.method, performance.now() - startedAt);
          };
          const reportFailure = (error: unknown): void => {
            try { sock.write(encodeFrame(singleErr(req.id, "execution_failed", error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)))); } catch { /* disconnected */ }
          };

          if (['graph.validate', 'graph.start', 'graph.submit', 'graph.status', 'graph.result', 'graph.cancel', 'graph.reconcile', 'graph.resume'].includes(req.method)) {
            void getGraphRpc().then(service => service.handle(sock, req.method, req.params)).then(result => {
              try { sock.write(encodeFrame(singleOk(req.id, result))); } catch { /* disconnected */ }
            }).catch(async error => {
              const { graphRpcError } = await import('./graph-rpc.js');
              const failure = graphRpcError(error);
              try { sock.write(encodeFrame(singleErr(req.id, failure.code, failure.message))); } catch { /* disconnected */ }
            }).finally(recordLatency);
            continue;
          }
          if (req.method === "task.submit") {
            void traceSpan(
              "osstack.task.submit",
              { "osstack.method": req.method },
              () => handleTaskSubmit(ctx, req, sock),
            ).catch(reportFailure).finally(recordLatency);
            continue;
          }
          if (req.method === "task.observe") {
            void traceSpan(
              "osstack.task.observe",
              { "osstack.method": req.method },
              async () => handleTaskObserve(ctx, req, sock),
            ).catch(reportFailure).finally(recordLatency);
            continue;
          }

          void traceSpan(
            "osstack.rpc",
            { "osstack.method": req.method },
            async () => {
              const resp = await handleSingle(ctx, req);
              if (resp === null) return;
              try { sock.write(encodeFrame(resp)); } catch { /* socket gone */ }
            },
          ).catch(reportFailure).finally(recordLatency);
        }
      } catch (err) {
        const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
        try { sock.write(encodeFrame({ id: 0, ok: false, error: { code: "framing", message } })); } catch { /* socket dead */ }
        sock.destroy();
      }
    });

    sock.on("error", () => { /* don't crash on client hangups */ });
  });

  failedServer = server;
  ctx.collaborator?.recover();
  return await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    server.once("error", reject);
    void Promise.all([restoreLoopSessions(ctx), restoreDailySessions(ctx)]).then(() => {
      server.listen(socketPath, () => {
      try { chmodSync(socketPath, 0o600); } catch { /* best effort */ }

      let healthPollPending = true;
      let guardedRecovery = refreshAutonomyHealthAndRecover(
        ctx,
        true,
        opts.notificationSender,
      )
        .finally(() => {
          healthPollPending = false;
        });
      const healthPollIntervalMs = Math.max(
        10,
        opts.healthPollIntervalMs ?? 30_000,
      );
      const wallClockMs = opts.wallClockMs ?? Date.now;
      const monotonicClockMs =
        opts.monotonicClockMs ?? (() => performance.now());
      const clockDetector = new ClockJumpDetector(
        opts.clockJumpThresholdMs ?? 120_000,
      );
      clockDetector.observe(wallClockMs(), monotonicClockMs());
      const healthMonitor =
        ctx.guardedAutonomy === undefined
          && opts.notificationSender === undefined
          ? undefined
          : setInterval(() => {
              if (healthPollPending) return;
              healthPollPending = true;
              const clock = clockDetector.observe(
                wallClockMs(),
                monotonicClockMs(),
              );
              if (clock.jump_detected) {
                process.stderr.write(
                  `osstack: clock jump detected drift_ms=${clock.drift_ms}; re-running external health and recovery probes\n`,
                );
              }
              guardedRecovery = refreshAutonomyHealthAndRecover(
                ctx,
                clock.jump_detected,
                opts.notificationSender,
              )
                .catch((error) => {
                  process.stderr.write(
                    `osstack: autonomy recovery monitor failed: ${
                      error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)
                    }\n`,
                  );
                })
                .finally(() => {
                  healthPollPending = false;
                });
            }, healthPollIntervalMs);
      healthMonitor?.unref();
      let shutdownPromise: Promise<void> | undefined;
      const shutdown = async (): Promise<void> => {
        checkpointClosed = true;
        if (checkpointService) {
          try { (await checkpointService).close(); } catch { /* Initialization failure created no service. */ }
        }
        if (shutdownPromise !== undefined) return await shutdownPromise;
        process.off("SIGTERM", onSig);
        process.off("SIGINT", onSig);
        try { stopUpdateChecker(); } catch { /* cleanup must continue */ }
        if (healthMonitor !== undefined) clearInterval(healthMonitor);
        shutdownPromise = (async () => {
          ctx.draining = true;
          await ctx.supervision?.stop();
          if (graphRpc) {
            try { await (await graphRpc).close(); } catch { /* Initialization may have failed. */ }
          }
          if (graphRuntime) {
            try { const runtime = await graphRuntime; await runtime.close(); await runtime.drainDatabaseUsers(); } catch { /* Failed initialization launched no graph worker. */ }
          }
          const collaboratorStopped = Promise.resolve().then(() => ctx.collaborator?.stop());
          // Abort any active runs so adapters get a chance to clean up.
          for (const ac of ctx.activeRuns.values()) ac.abort();
          for (const session of ctx.loopSessions.values()) {
            session.shutdownRequested = true;
            session.abort.abort();
          }
          for (const session of ctx.dailySessions.values()) {
            session.shutdownRequested = true;
            session.abort.abort();
          }
          const loopCompletions = Array.from(ctx.loopSessions.values())
            .map((session) => session.done)
            .filter((done): done is Promise<void> => done !== undefined);
          const dailyCompletions = Array.from(ctx.dailySessions.values())
            .map((session) => session.done)
            .filter((done): done is Promise<void> => done !== undefined);
          await Promise.allSettled([
            ...loopCompletions,
            ...dailyCompletions,
            guardedRecovery,
            collaboratorStopped,
          ]);
          await new Promise<void>((resolveClose) => server.close(() => resolveClose()));
          try { if (dashboard !== null) await dashboard.close(); }
          finally {
            try { ctx.db.close(); }
            finally {
              try { cleanupStaleSocket(socketPath); } catch { /* diagnostics only */ }
              try { await shutdownOtel(); } finally { releaseLock(); }
            }
          }
        })();
        return await shutdownPromise;
      };
      const onSig = (): void => { void shutdown().then(() => process.exit(0)); };
      process.once("SIGTERM", onSig);
      process.once("SIGINT", onSig);
      ctx.requestShutdown = onSig;

      const dashOpts = dashboardUrlFromEnv();
      void startDashboardServer(
        () => createDashboardSnapshot({
          db: ctx.db,
          kanban: ctx.kanban,
          dbPath: ctx.dbPath,
          version: DAEMON_VERSION,
          phase: DAEMON_PHASE,
          dashboardUrl: ctx.dashboardUrl ?? dashboardUrl(dashOpts),
          adapters: Array.from(ctx.adapters.values()).map((a) => ({
            id: a.id,
            display_name: a.display_name,
            cost_tier: a.cost_tier,
          })),
          activeRuns: ctx.activeRuns.size,
          activeLoops: activeLoopCount(ctx),
        }),
        dashOpts,
      ).then((handle) => {
        dashboard = handle;
        ctx.dashboardUrl = handle.url;
      }).catch((err) => {
        process.stderr.write(`osstack: dashboard server failed: ${(err as Error).message}\n`);
      });

      resolve({ close: shutdown, socketPath, dbPath: ctx.dbPath,
        executeGraphCheckpoint: async (connection, request) => (await getCheckpoints()).execute(connection, request),
        graphActionBroker: getBroker,
        graphRuntime: (configuration) => {
          if (checkpointClosed) return Promise.reject(new Error("GRAPH_RUNTIME_CLOSED"));
          if (graphRuntime) return Promise.reject(new Error("GRAPH_RUNTIME_ALREADY_CONFIGURED"));
          // Snapshot trusted launch settings before dynamic import. Program bytes are
          // verified/snapshotted by the manager; the SDK stays in its child process.
          const fixed = structuredClone(configuration);
          graphRuntime = Promise.all([import("./graph-runtime.js"), getBroker(), getCheckpoints()])
            .then(async ([{ GraphRuntime }, broker, checkpoints]) => {
              const runtime = new GraphRuntime({ ...fixed, db: ctx.db, broker, checkpoints });
              if (checkpointClosed) { await runtime.close(); throw new Error("GRAPH_RUNTIME_CLOSED"); }
              return runtime;
            });
          return graphRuntime;
        },
        acquireGraphAdmission: async (identity, input) => {
          const trusted = opts.graphIdentities?.get(identity);
          if (!trusted || typeof trusted.authorize !== "function") throw new Error("trusted graph identity and authorization required");
          return admissionFor(ctx).acquire({ ...input, kind: "write",
            workspacePath: trusted.workspacePath, authorize: async () => {
              const decision = await makeRepositoryPolicyGate(trusted.workspacePath, true)({ workspacePath: trusted.workspacePath });
              if (!decision.allow) throw new Error(decision.reason);
              await trusted.authorize();
            } });
        },
      });
      });
    }).catch(reject);
  });
  } catch (error) {
    // Preserve the startup error; every acquired resource gets its own cleanup.
    try { stopUpdates?.(); } catch { /* continue cleanup */ }
    if (failedContext) {
      failedContext.draining = true;
      await failedContext.supervision?.stop();
      const stopped = Promise.resolve().then(() => failedContext!.collaborator?.stop());
      for (const session of [...failedContext.dailySessions.values(), ...failedContext.loopSessions.values()]) {
        session.shutdownRequested = true; session.abort.abort();
      }
      await Promise.allSettled([stopped, ...[...failedContext.dailySessions.values(), ...failedContext.loopSessions.values()].map((session) => session.done)]);
    }
    try { if (failedServer) await new Promise<void>((done) => failedServer!.close(() => done())); } catch { /* unbound */ }
    try { failedContext?.db.close(); } catch { /* continue cleanup */ }
    try { await shutdownOtel(); } catch { /* preserve original error */ }
    finally { releaseLock(); }
    throw error;
  }
}


FINAL SOURCE packages/cli/src/governed-execution.ts
import { join } from "node:path";
import { acquireRepoWriteLock, FifoSemaphore, SemaphoreAcquireAbortedError, type SemaphoreLease, type RepoWriteLock } from '@osstack/autonomy';
import { resolveRepositoryScope } from '@osstack/core';

export interface ExecutionLease {
  release(): Promise<void>;
  /** Keep ownership until a trusted caller has confirmed external exit. */
  retain(reason: string): void;
  /** Trusted host only; never accept a worker-provided proof callback. */
  releaseAfterReconciliation?(verify: () => Promise<void>): Promise<void>;
  /** Assert this exact canonical writer lease is still live. */
  assertActive?(): Promise<void>;
  /** Scoped synchronous file operations under the canonical writer lock. This
   * capability is absent on read leases. Failures retain admission until recovery. */
  withWriteGuard?<T>(workspacePath: string, operation: (assertActive: () => void) => T): Promise<T>;
}
export interface AdmissionInput {
  kind: 'read' | 'write';
  runId: string;
  /** Supplied by the daemon registration, never by an adapter context. */
  workspacePath?: string;
  repoId?: string;
  signal?: AbortSignal;
  authorize?: () => Promise<void>;
  /** Host-only synchronous proof: acquisition rejected with no lease or cleanup obligation. */
  onNotAcquired?: () => void;
}
export class GovernedExecution {
  private readonly activeRuns = new Set<string>();
  private readonly quarantinedRepositories = new Map<string, Set<string>>();
  private quarantine(repoId: string, runId: string): void {
    const owners = this.quarantinedRepositories.get(repoId) ?? new Set<string>();
    owners.add(runId); this.quarantinedRepositories.set(repoId, owners);
  }
  constructor(private readonly options: {
    reads: FifoSemaphore;
    writes: FifoSemaphore;
    repositories: Map<string, FifoSemaphore>;
    checkHealth: () => Promise<void>;
  }) {}

  get hasWriters(): boolean { return this.options.writes.in_use > 0; }

  async acquire(input: AdmissionInput): Promise<ExecutionLease> {
    const cleanup = { confirmed: true };
    try { return await this.acquireOwned(input, cleanup); }
    catch (error) {
      if (cleanup.confirmed && !this.activeRuns.has(input.runId)) input.onNotAcquired?.();
      throw error;
    }
  }

  private async acquireOwned(input: AdmissionInput, cleanup: { confirmed: boolean }): Promise<ExecutionLease> {
    const check = async (): Promise<void> => {
      if (input.signal?.aborted) throw new SemaphoreAcquireAbortedError();
      if (input.kind === 'write') await this.options.checkHealth();
      await input.authorize?.();
    };
    await check();
    const scope = input.workspacePath === undefined ? undefined
      : await resolveRepositoryScope(input.workspacePath, input.repoId === undefined ? {} : { repoId: input.repoId });
    if (input.kind === 'write' && !scope) throw new Error('trusted repository scope required for writes');
    if (input.kind === "write" && scope && this.quarantinedRepositories.has(scope.repoId)) throw new Error("repository external stop unconfirmed; reconciliation required");
    if (this.activeRuns.has(input.runId)) {
      cleanup.confirmed = false;
      throw new Error("run already admitted or queued");
    }
    this.activeRuns.add(input.runId);
    let local: SemaphoreLease | undefined;
    let global: SemaphoreLease | undefined;
    let lock: RepoWriteLock | undefined;
    let queue: FifoSemaphore | undefined;
    const releasePermits = (): void => {
      global?.release(); local?.release();
      this.activeRuns.delete(input.runId);
      if (scope && queue?.in_use === 0 && queue.pending === 0) this.options.repositories.delete(scope.repoId);
    };
    let released = false;
    let retained = false;
    let writeGuardActive = false;
    // Start as soon as the filesystem lock exists, including the final policy
    // wait. Once admitted, a renewal failure retains the unknown producer.
    let renewing: Promise<void> = Promise.resolve();
    let timer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | undefined;
    const startRenewal = () => {
      timer = setInterval(() => {
        if (writeGuardActive) return;
        renewing = renewing.then(async () => {
          if (!released && !writeGuardActive) await lock!.renew();
        }).catch((error: unknown) => {
          retained = true;
          if (scope) this.quarantine(scope.repoId, input.runId);
          process.stderr.write(`repository lease renewal failed; retained ${input.runId}: ${String(error)}\n`);
          if (timer) clearInterval(timer);
        });
      }, 20_000);
      timer.unref();
    };
    try {
      if (input.kind === 'write' && scope) {
        queue = this.options.repositories.get(scope.repoId) ?? new FifoSemaphore(1);
        this.options.repositories.set(scope.repoId, queue);
        local = await queue.acquire(input.signal);
      }
      global = await (input.kind === 'read' ? this.options.reads : this.options.writes).acquire(input.signal);
      if (input.kind === 'write' && scope) {
        // A failed filesystem acquisition may have left an ownership obligation.
        cleanup.confirmed = false;
        lock = await acquireRepoWriteLock({ root_dir: join(scope.commonDir, "osstack", "writer-locks"), recover_stale: false, repo_id: scope.repoId, autonomy_run_id: input.runId });
        // Authorization may wait behind another branch's synchronous work.
        // Start ownership renewal before that wait, not after publishing admission.
        startRenewal();
      }
      if (input.kind === "write" && scope && this.quarantinedRepositories.has(scope.repoId)) throw new Error("repository external stop unconfirmed; reconciliation required");
      await check();
      await renewing;
      if (retained) throw new Error("repository lease renewal failed during admission");
      await lock?.assertActive();
      if (retained) throw new Error("repository lease renewal failed during admission");
      if (input.signal?.aborted) throw new SemaphoreAcquireAbortedError();
    } catch (error) {
      if (timer) clearInterval(timer);
      await renewing;
      try {
        await lock?.release();
        if (lock) cleanup.confirmed = true;
        // No executor was admitted. Remove only this rejected acquisition's quarantine.
        if (scope) {
          const owners = this.quarantinedRepositories.get(scope.repoId);
          owners?.delete(input.runId);
          if (owners?.size === 0) this.quarantinedRepositories.delete(scope.repoId);
        }
      } catch (releaseError) {
        if (scope) this.quarantine(scope.repoId, input.runId);
        throw new AggregateError([error, releaseError], 'admission rejected and ownership cleanup failed');
      } finally { releasePermits(); }
      throw error;
    }
    let reconciled: Promise<void> | undefined;
    return {
      ...(lock && scope ? { withWriteGuard: async <T>(workspacePath: string, operation: (assertActive: () => void) => T): Promise<T> => {
        if (workspacePath !== scope.workspacePath) throw new Error('write guard workspace mismatch');
        if (released || retained || writeGuardActive || reconciled) throw new Error('execution lease unavailable');
        writeGuardActive = true;
        try {
          await renewing;
          if (released || retained) throw new Error('execution lease unavailable');
          // Do not let the timer contend with our metadata guard. Renew on both
          // sides while still excluding release; renewal never revives expiry.
          await lock!.renew();
          const value = await lock!.withActiveGuard(check => {
            const assertActive = () => {
              if (released || retained) throw new Error('execution lease unavailable');
              check();
            };
            assertActive(); const result = operation(assertActive);
            if (result !== null && (typeof result === 'object' || typeof result === 'function') && 'then' in result) {
              void Promise.resolve(result).catch(() => {});
              throw new Error('write guard operation must be synchronous');
            }
            assertActive(); return result;
          });
          await lock!.renew();
          return value;
        } catch (error) {
          retained = true; if (timer) clearInterval(timer);
          this.quarantine(scope.repoId, input.runId);
          throw error;
        } finally { writeGuardActive = false; }
      } } : {}),
      assertActive: async () => {
        if (released || retained) throw new Error("execution lease unavailable");
        await lock?.assertActive();
        if (released || retained) throw new Error("execution lease unavailable");
      },
      releaseAfterReconciliation: (verify) => {
        if (writeGuardActive) return Promise.reject(new Error('write guard in progress'));
        if (released) return Promise.resolve();
        if (reconciled) return reconciled;
        if (typeof verify !== 'function') return Promise.reject(new Error('trusted reconciliation verifier required'));
        reconciled = (async () => {
          // Stop renewal first. No authority is freed until the verifier succeeds
          // and the canonical token is released under its filesystem guard.
          if (timer) clearInterval(timer);
          await renewing;
          const result: unknown = await verify();
          if (result !== undefined) throw new Error('invalid reconciliation authority');
          await lock?.release();
          released = true; retained = false;
          if (scope) {
            const owners = this.quarantinedRepositories.get(scope.repoId);
            owners?.delete(input.runId);
            if (owners?.size === 0) this.quarantinedRepositories.delete(scope.repoId);
          }
          releasePermits();
        })().catch((error: unknown) => {
          retained = true;
          if (scope) this.quarantine(scope.repoId, input.runId);
          throw error;
        }).finally(() => { reconciled = undefined; });
        return reconciled;
      },
      retain: (reason) => { retained = true; if (timer) clearInterval(timer); if (scope) this.quarantine(scope.repoId, input.runId); process.stderr.write(`osstack: retaining write lease ${input.runId}: ${reason}\n`); },
      release: async () => {
        if (writeGuardActive) throw new Error('write guard in progress');
        if (released) return;
        if (retained) throw new Error("external stop unconfirmed; reconciliation required before release");
        released = true;
        if (timer) clearInterval(timer);
        await renewing;
        if (retained) { released = false; throw new Error("external stop unconfirmed; reconciliation required before release"); }
        // Ownership failure must not free the in-process writer authority.
        try { await lock?.release(); }
        catch (error) {
          released = false; retained = true;
          if (scope) this.quarantine(scope.repoId, input.runId);
          process.stderr.write(`repository lock release failed; retained ${input.runId}: ${String(error)}\n`);
          throw error;
        }
        releasePermits();
      },
    };
  }
}


FINAL SOURCE packages/core/src/state/db.ts
// SQLite connection management.
//
// Decisions locked in plan v0.6:
//   - better-sqlite3 (synchronous, single connection per daemon process)
//   - WAL mode for concurrent reader/writer safety
//   - synchronous=NORMAL (WAL-safe, faster than FULL)
//   - 64 MiB page cache
//   - foreign_keys ON (so ON DELETE CASCADE works for runs/events on task delete)
//
// CRITICAL GAP G1 (plan v0.6 §8.3): disk-full must never cause a silent task
// write loss. Every write goes through preflightDiskSpace(), which throws
// DiskFullError before any INSERT/UPDATE can corrupt or stall a transaction.

import Database from "better-sqlite3";
import { statfsSync } from "node:fs";
import { dirname } from "node:path";
import { CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION, MIGRATIONS } from "./migrations.js";

/** Default safety threshold for disk-full preflight (100 MiB). */
export const DEFAULT_MIN_FREE_BYTES = 100 * 1024 * 1024;

/** Cache size in -kibibyte units. negative = KiB (per SQLite docs). 64 MiB. */
const PAGE_CACHE_KIB = -65536;

export class DiskFullError extends Error {
  readonly path: string;
  readonly freeBytes: number;
  readonly minFreeBytes: number;
  constructor(path: string, freeBytes: number, minFreeBytes: number) {
    super(
      `Disk full preflight failed for ${path}: ${freeBytes} bytes free, ` +
      `need ≥${minFreeBytes}. Refusing write so Hermes can be notified.`,
    );
    this.name = "DiskFullError";
    this.path = path;
    this.freeBytes = freeBytes;
    this.minFreeBytes = minFreeBytes;
  }
}

export interface OpenDbOptions {
  /** Absolute path to the SQLite file (parent dir must exist). */
  path: string;
  /** When false, opens read-only. */
  readwrite?: boolean;
  /** Override the disk-full safety threshold (bytes). */
  minFreeBytes?: number;
}

/**
 * Run a free-space check against the filesystem holding `path`. Throws
 * DiskFullError if below threshold; otherwise returns free byte count.
 * Skips the check (returns Infinity) if statfsSync is unsupported on the
 * platform — better to allow writes than to crash a daemon on a kernel quirk.
 */
export function preflightDiskSpace(
  path: string,
  minFreeBytes: number = DEFAULT_MIN_FREE_BYTES,
): number {
  let stats: { bsize: number; bavail: number };
  try {
    stats = statfsSync(dirname(path));
  } catch {
    return Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
  }
  const free = Number(stats.bsize) * Number(stats.bavail);
  if (free < minFreeBytes) throw new DiskFullError(path, free, minFreeBytes);
  return free;
}

/**
 * Open the osstack state database, apply pragmas, and run pending migrations.
 * Caller owns the lifetime — pair every openDb() with a db.close() in shutdown.
 */
export function openDb(opts: OpenDbOptions): Database.Database {
  const { path, readwrite = true, minFreeBytes = DEFAULT_MIN_FREE_BYTES } = opts;
  if (readwrite) preflightDiskSpace(path, minFreeBytes);

  const db = new Database(path, { readonly: !readwrite });

  try {
    // Refuse an unsupported schema before changing journal mode or running DDL,
    // including read-only inspection. A failed open never escapes with a live handle.
    assertSupportedVersion(db.pragma("user_version", { simple: true }) as number);
    assertMigrationLineage(db);
    // Pragmas are applied per-connection in SQLite. Apply on every open.
    if (readwrite) db.pragma("journal_mode = WAL");
    db.pragma("synchronous = NORMAL");
    db.pragma(`cache_size = ${PAGE_CACHE_KIB}`);
    db.pragma("foreign_keys = ON");
    db.pragma("busy_timeout = 5000");

    if (readwrite) runMigrations(db);
    return db;
  } catch (error) {
    db.close();
    throw error;
  }
}

// This schema is table-additive. Derive expected table introductions once;
// a future DROP/RENAME migration must also update this lineage contract.
const tableIntroductions = MIGRATIONS.flatMap(migration =>
  [...migration.sql.matchAll(/^\s*CREATE\s+TABLE(?:\s+IF\s+NOT\s+EXISTS)?\s+([a-zA-Z_]\w*)\s*\(/gm)]
    .map(match => ({ name: match[1]!, version: migration.id })));

/** Reject pure or mixed unreleased Graph schemas before application pragmas or
 * DDL. SQLite may itself checkpoint an abandoned WAL on close; logical ledger
 * content and user_version must remain intact for the original binary. */
function assertMigrationLineage(db: Database.Database): void {
  const current = db.pragma("user_version", { simple: true }) as number;
  const actual = new Set((db.prepare("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'").all() as { name: string }[]).map(row => row.name));
  // Earlier main versions are unaffected unless an experimental Graph touched them.
  if (current < 25 && ![...actual].some(name => name.startsWith("graph_"))) return;
  const expected = new Set(tableIntroductions.filter(table => table.version <= current).map(table => table.name));
  if ([...expected].some(name => !actual.has(name))
    || [...actual].some(name => name.startsWith("graph_") && !expected.has(name))) {
    throw new Error("GRAPH_EXPERIMENTAL_SCHEMA_CONFLICT: preserve this home and reconcile with its original build; do not reset user_version or reuse it for the integrated runtime");
  }
}

function assertSupportedVersion(current: number): void {
  if (current > CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION) {
    throw new Error(`Migration state inconsistent: user_version=${current}, expected=${CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION}`);
  }
}

/**
 * Apply pending migrations idempotently. `user_version` PRAGMA tracks progress.
 * The version pragma guarantees forward-only application. Many migrations
 * contain non-idempotent CREATE/ALTER statements; never reset user_version.
 */
export function runMigrations(db: Database.Database): void {
  const currentRow = db.pragma("user_version", { simple: true }) as number;
  let current = Number(currentRow);
  assertSupportedVersion(current);
  assertMigrationLineage(db);
  for (const m of MIGRATIONS) {
    if (m.id <= current) continue;
    db.exec("BEGIN");
    try {
      db.exec(m.sql);
      db.pragma(`user_version = ${m.id}`);
      db.exec("COMMIT");
      current = m.id;
    } catch (err) {
      db.exec("ROLLBACK");
      throw err;
    }
  }
  if (current !== CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION) {
    throw new Error(
      `Migration state inconsistent: user_version=${current}, expected=${CURRENT_SCHEMA_VERSION}`,
    );
  }
}

/**
 * Run a function inside a transaction with preflight. Use for any write path
 * called from the daemon hot loop. Wraps better-sqlite3's transaction() so
 * disk-space errors fire BEFORE BEGIN, not mid-transaction.
 */
export function withWrite<T>(
  db: Database.Database,
  path: string,
  fn: (db: Database.Database) => T,
  minFreeBytes: number = DEFAULT_MIN_FREE_BYTES,
): T {
  preflightDiskSpace(path, minFreeBytes);
  const tx = db.transaction(fn);
  return tx(db);
}


FINAL SOURCE packages/core/src/state/sessions.ts
import type Database from "better-sqlite3";
import { withWrite } from "./db.js";
import {
  AgentSessionRow,
  AgentSessionState,
  SessionMessageRow,
  SessionTurnRow,
  type AgentSessionRow as AgentSessionRowType,
  type AgentSessionState as AgentSessionStateType,
  type SessionMessageRow as SessionMessageRowType,
  type SessionTurnRow as SessionTurnRowType,
} from "./schema.js";

type RawSessionRow = Omit<AgentSessionRowType, "resume_supported"> & { resume_supported: number };

export interface SessionTurnInput extends Omit<SessionTurnRowType, "decision_json"> {
  decision: unknown | null;
}

export interface SessionMessageInput extends Omit<SessionMessageRowType, "payload_json" | "applied_at"> {
  payload: unknown;
  applied_at?: number | null;
}

export interface SessionFilter {
  agent_id?: string;
  state?: AgentSessionStateType;
  task_id?: string;
  limit?: number;
}

export interface ApplyMessageResult {
  outcome: "applied" | "duplicate";
  session_version: number;
}

export interface CompleteInboundTurnInput {
  turn: SessionTurnInput;
  message_id: string;
  expected_version: number;
  applied_at: number;
}

export interface SessionSnapshot {
  session_id: string;
  task_id: string;
  run_id: string;
  agent_id: string;
  runtime_id: string;
  state: AgentSessionStateType;
  version: number;
  turn_id: string | null;
  assistant_text: string;
  decision: unknown | null;
  updated_at: number;
}

export class SessionVersionConflict extends Error {
  constructor(
    readonly session_id: string,
    readonly expected_version: number,
    readonly actual_version: number | null,
  ) {
    super(
      `session ${session_id} version conflict: expected ${expected_version}, `
      + `actual ${actual_version ?? "missing"}`,
    );
    this.name = "SessionVersionConflict";
  }
}

export class SessionMessageIdConflict extends Error {
  constructor(readonly message_id: string) {
    super(`message_id collision for ${message_id}`);
    this.name = "SessionMessageIdConflict";
  }
}

function parseSession(row: RawSessionRow): AgentSessionRowType {
  return AgentSessionRow.parse({ ...row, resume_supported: row.resume_supported === 1 });
}

function canonicalJson(value: unknown): string {
  if (value === null || typeof value !== "object") return JSON.stringify(value);
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return `[${value.map(canonicalJson).join(",")}]`;
  const entries = Object.entries(value as Record<string, unknown>)
    .sort(([left], [right]) => left.localeCompare(right));
  return `{${entries.map(([key, item]) => `${JSON.stringify(key)}:${canonicalJson(item)}`).join(",")}}`;
}

export class SessionRepository {
  constructor(
    private readonly db: Database.Database,
    private readonly path: string,
  ) {}

  createSession(row: AgentSessionRowType): void {
    const parsed = AgentSessionRow.parse(row);
    withWrite(this.db, this.path, (db) => {
      db.prepare(`
        INSERT INTO agent_sessions(
          session_id,task_id,run_id,agent_id,request_sha256,native_session_id,
          repo_path,state,resume_supported,version,updated_at
        ) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)
      `).run(
        parsed.session_id, parsed.task_id, parsed.run_id, parsed.agent_id,
        parsed.request_sha256, parsed.native_session_id, parsed.repo_path, parsed.state,
        parsed.resume_supported ? 1 : 0, parsed.version, parsed.updated_at,
      );
    });
  }

  getSession(sessionId: string): AgentSessionRowType | undefined {
    const row = this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM agent_sessions WHERE session_id = ?")
      .get(sessionId) as RawSessionRow | undefined;
    return row ? parseSession(row) : undefined;
  }

  recordNativeSession(
    sessionId: string,
    nativeSessionId: string,
    expectedVersion: number,
    updatedAt: number,
  ): AgentSessionRowType {
    return this.updateSession(
      sessionId,
      expectedVersion,
      updatedAt,
      "native_session_id = ?, state = 'idle'",
      [nativeSessionId],
    );
  }

  setState(
    sessionId: string,
    state: AgentSessionStateType,
    expectedVersion: number,
    updatedAt: number,
  ): AgentSessionRowType {
    AgentSessionState.parse(state);
    return this.updateSession(sessionId, expectedVersion, updatedAt, "state = ?", [state]);
  }

  appendTurn(input: SessionTurnInput): void {
    const { decision, ...stored } = input;
    const row = SessionTurnRow.parse({
      ...stored,
      decision_json: decision === null ? null : canonicalJson(decision),
    });
    withWrite(this.db, this.path, (db) => {
      db.prepare(`
        INSERT INTO session_turns(
          turn_id,session_id,ordinal,state,assistant_text,decision_json,created_at,updated_at
        ) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)
      `).run(
        row.turn_id, row.session_id, row.ordinal, row.state, row.assistant_text,
        row.decision_json, row.created_at, row.updated_at,
      );
    });
  }

  applyInbound(
    input: SessionMessageInput,
    expectedVersion: number,
    appliedAt: number,
  ): ApplyMessageResult {
    const { payload, ...stored } = input;
    const payloadJson = canonicalJson(payload);
    const row = SessionMessageRow.parse({
      ...stored,
      payload_json: payloadJson,
      applied_at: input.applied_at ?? null,
    });

    return withWrite(this.db, this.path, (db) => {
      const existing = db.prepare("SELECT * FROM session_messages WHERE message_id = ?")
        .get(row.message_id) as SessionMessageRowType | undefined;
      if (existing) {
        if (
          existing.session_id !== row.session_id
          || existing.turn_id !== row.turn_id
          || existing.kind !== row.kind
          || existing.payload_json !== payloadJson
        ) {
          throw new SessionMessageIdConflict(row.message_id);
        }
        const session = this.readSession(db, row.session_id);
        return { outcome: "duplicate", session_version: session.version };
      }

      const result = db.prepare(`
        UPDATE agent_sessions
        SET version = version + 1, updated_at = ?
        WHERE session_id = ? AND version = ?
      `).run(appliedAt, row.session_id, expectedVersion);
      if (result.changes !== 1) {
        const actual = db.prepare("SELECT version FROM agent_sessions WHERE session_id = ?")
          .get(row.session_id) as { version: number } | undefined;
        throw new SessionVersionConflict(row.session_id, expectedVersion, actual?.version ?? null);
      }

      db.prepare(`
        INSERT INTO session_messages(
          message_id,session_id,turn_id,direction,kind,payload_json,state,created_at,applied_at
        ) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)
      `).run(
        row.message_id, row.session_id, row.turn_id, row.direction, row.kind,
        payloadJson, row.state, row.created_at, row.applied_at,
      );
      return { outcome: "applied", session_version: expectedVersion + 1 };
    });
  }

  markMessageApplied(messageId: string, appliedAt: number): void {
    withWrite(this.db, this.path, (db) => {
      const result = db.prepare(`
        UPDATE session_messages
        SET state = 'applied', applied_at = ?
        WHERE message_id = ? AND state = 'accepted'
      `).run(appliedAt, messageId);
      if (result.changes !== 1) {
        throw new Error(`message ${messageId} is missing or is not accepted`);
      }
    });
  }

  markMessageFailed(messageId: string): void {
    withWrite(this.db, this.path, (db) => {
      const result = db.prepare(`
        UPDATE session_messages
        SET state = 'failed', applied_at = NULL
        WHERE message_id = ? AND state = 'accepted'
      `).run(messageId);
      if (result.changes !== 1) {
        throw new Error(`message ${messageId} is missing or is not accepted`);
      }
    });
  }

  retryFailedMessage(messageId: string): void {
    withWrite(this.db, this.path, (db) => {
      const result = db.prepare(`
        UPDATE session_messages
        SET state = 'accepted', applied_at = NULL
        WHERE message_id = ? AND state = 'failed'
      `).run(messageId);
      if (result.changes !== 1) {
        throw new Error(`message ${messageId} is missing or is not failed`);
      }
    });
  }

  completeInboundTurn(input: CompleteInboundTurnInput): AgentSessionRowType {
    AgentSessionState.parse(input.turn.state);
    const { decision, ...storedTurn } = input.turn;
    const turn = SessionTurnRow.parse({
      ...storedTurn,
      decision_json: decision === null ? null : canonicalJson(decision),
    });

    return withWrite(this.db, this.path, (db) => {
      const message = db.prepare(`
        SELECT session_id, state FROM session_messages WHERE message_id = ?
      `).get(input.message_id) as {
        session_id: string;
        state: SessionMessageRowType["state"];
      } | undefined;
      if (
        message === undefined
        || message.session_id !== turn.session_id
        || message.state !== "accepted"
      ) {
        throw new Error(
          `message ${input.message_id} is missing, belongs to another session, or is not accepted`,
        );
      }

      const updated = db.prepare(`
        UPDATE agent_sessions
        SET state = ?, version = version + 1, updated_at = ?
        WHERE session_id = ? AND version = ?
      `).run(
        turn.state,
        input.applied_at,
        turn.session_id,
        input.expected_version,
      );
      if (updated.changes !== 1) {
        const actual = db.prepare("SELECT version FROM agent_sessions WHERE session_id = ?")
          .get(turn.session_id) as { version: number } | undefined;
        throw new SessionVersionConflict(
          turn.session_id,
          input.expected_version,
          actual?.version ?? null,
        );
      }

      db.prepare(`
        INSERT INTO session_turns(
          turn_id,session_id,ordinal,state,assistant_text,decision_json,created_at,updated_at
        ) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)
      `).run(
        turn.turn_id,
        turn.session_id,
        turn.ordinal,
        turn.state,
        turn.assistant_text,
        turn.decision_json,
        turn.created_at,
        turn.updated_at,
      );

      const applied = db.prepare(`
        UPDATE session_messages
        SET state = 'applied', applied_at = ?
        WHERE message_id = ? AND state = 'accepted'
      `).run(input.applied_at, input.message_id);
      if (applied.changes !== 1) {
        throw new Error(`message ${input.message_id} could not be marked applied`);
      }
      return this.readSession(db, turn.session_id);
    });
  }

  getMessage(messageId: string): SessionMessageRowType | undefined {
    const row = this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM session_messages WHERE message_id = ?")
      .get(messageId) as SessionMessageRowType | undefined;
    return row ? SessionMessageRow.parse(row) : undefined;
  }

  latestSnapshot(sessionId: string): SessionSnapshot {
    const session = this.readSession(this.db, sessionId);
    const turn = this.db.prepare(`
      SELECT * FROM session_turns WHERE session_id = ? ORDER BY ordinal DESC LIMIT 1
    `).get(sessionId) as SessionTurnRowType | undefined;
    const parsedTurn = turn ? SessionTurnRow.parse(turn) : undefined;
    return {
      session_id: session.session_id,
      task_id: session.task_id,
      run_id: session.run_id,
      agent_id: session.agent_id,
      runtime_id: session.agent_id,
      state: session.state,
      version: session.version,
      turn_id: parsedTurn?.turn_id ?? null,
      assistant_text: parsedTurn?.assistant_text ?? "",
      decision: parsedTurn?.decision_json ? JSON.parse(parsedTurn.decision_json) : null,
      updated_at: session.updated_at,
    };
  }

  listSessions(filter: SessionFilter = {}): AgentSessionRowType[] {
    const where: string[] = [];
    const values: Array<string | number> = [];
    if (filter.agent_id !== undefined) {
      where.push("agent_id = ?");
      values.push(filter.agent_id);
    }
    if (filter.state !== undefined) {
      AgentSessionState.parse(filter.state);
      where.push("state = ?");
      values.push(filter.state);
    }
    if (filter.task_id !== undefined) {
      where.push("task_id = ?");
      values.push(filter.task_id);
    }
    const limit = filter.limit ?? 200;
    if (!Number.isInteger(limit) || limit < 1 || limit > 200) {
      throw new Error("session list limit must be an integer between 1 and 200");
    }
    values.push(limit);
    const sql = `SELECT * FROM agent_sessions${where.length > 0 ? ` WHERE ${where.join(" AND ")}` : ""} ORDER BY updated_at DESC, session_id LIMIT ?`;
    return (this.db.prepare(sql).all(...values) as RawSessionRow[]).map(parseSession);
  }

  private updateSession(
    sessionId: string,
    expectedVersion: number,
    updatedAt: number,
    assignment: string,
    values: unknown[],
  ): AgentSessionRowType {
    return withWrite(this.db, this.path, (db) => {
      const result = db.prepare(`
        UPDATE agent_sessions
        SET ${assignment}, version = version + 1, updated_at = ?
        WHERE session_id = ? AND version = ?
      `).run(...values, updatedAt, sessionId, expectedVersion);
      if (result.changes !== 1) {
        const actual = db.prepare("SELECT version FROM agent_sessions WHERE session_id = ?")
          .get(sessionId) as { version: number } | undefined;
        throw new SessionVersionConflict(sessionId, expectedVersion, actual?.version ?? null);
      }
      return this.readSession(db, sessionId);
    });
  }

  private readSession(db: Database.Database, sessionId: string): AgentSessionRowType {
    const row = db.prepare("SELECT * FROM agent_sessions WHERE session_id = ?")
      .get(sessionId) as RawSessionRow | undefined;
    if (!row) throw new SessionVersionConflict(sessionId, 0, null);
    return parseSession(row);
  }
}


FINAL SOURCE packages/cli/src/supervision-service.ts
import { AttentionInbox } from "@osstack/protocol";
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import type Database from "better-sqlite3";
import {
  AttentionStore,
  AttentionObserver,
  AttentionExperience,
  AttentionActions,
  attentionDigest,
  type AttentionSender,
} from "@osstack/core";
/** The daemon has already acquired its kernel process lock before construction. */
export class SupervisionService {
  readonly store: AttentionStore;
  readonly observer: AttentionObserver;
  readonly experience: AttentionExperience;
  private delivering = false;
  private deliveryDone: Promise<void> | undefined;
  private ciReader: import("@osstack/github").AttentionCiReader | undefined;
  private timer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | undefined;
  constructor(
    db: Database.Database,
    path: string,
    readonly sender?: AttentionSender
  ) {
    this.store = new AttentionStore(db, path);
    this.observer = new AttentionObserver(
      this.store,
      this.store.claimOwner(randomUUID())
    );
    this.experience = new AttentionExperience(this.store, this.observer.owner);
    this.experience.recoverDeliveries();
    new AttentionActions(this.store, this.observer.owner).recover();
  }
  start(): void {
    this.scan();
    this.timer = setInterval(() => this.scan(), 30000);
    this.timer.unref();
  }
  private scan(): void {
    try {
      this.observer.tick();
      const loops = this.store.db
        .prepare(
          "SELECT scope_id,subject_id,payload_json FROM attention_subjects WHERE kind='loop' AND admitted=1"
        )
        .all() as {
        scope_id: string;
        subject_id: string;
        payload_json: string;
      }[];
      for (const row of loops) {
        const path = JSON.parse(row.payload_json).state_path;
        void this.handle("attention.loop", {
          scope_id: row.scope_id,
          loop_id: row.subject_id,
          state_path: path,
        }).catch(() => {
          /* previous sample expires */
        });
      }
      for (const scope of this.store.listScopes())
        this.experience.enqueue(scope.scope_id);
      if (this.sender && !this.delivering) {
        this.delivering = true;
        this.deliveryDone = this.experience
          .drain(this.sender)
          .catch(() => {
            /* delivery state retained */
          })
          .finally(() => {
            this.delivering = false;
          });
      }
    } catch {
      /* coverage expires even if timer is alive */
    }
  }
  async stop(): Promise<void> {
    if (this.timer) clearInterval(this.timer);
    this.timer = undefined;
    await this.deliveryDone;
  }
  async handle(method: string, params: unknown): Promise<unknown> {
    if (!params || typeof params !== "object")
      throw new Error("explicit_scope_required");
    const p = params as Record<string, unknown>;
    const str = (key: string) => {
      const value = p[key];
      if (typeof value !== "string" || !value || value.length > 4096)
        throw new Error(`invalid_${key}`);
      return value;
    };
    if (method === "attention.watch") {
      if (p["default_tenant"] !== true)
        throw new Error("explicit_default_tenant_required");
      const scope = this.store.registerScope(
        this.observer.owner,
        str("task_id"),
        str("repo_path")
      );
      this.observer.tick();
      return this.observer.inventory(scope.scope_id);
    }
    const scopeId = str("scope_id");
    if (!this.store.getScope(scopeId)) throw new Error("not_found");
    if (method === "attention.release") {
      this.store.release(
        this.observer.owner,
        scopeId,
        str("kind"),
        str("subject_id"),
        Number(p["expected_version"])
      );
      return {
        released: true,
        execution_affected: false,
        coverage: "uncovered",
      };
    }
    if (method === "attention.loop") {
      const { readAttentionLoop } = await import("./attention-loop-source.js");
      const path = str("state_path");
      const id = str("loop_id");
      const facts = await readAttentionLoop({
        repo: this.store.getScope(scopeId)!.repo_path,
        path,
        loop_id: id,
      });
      this.store.write(this.observer.owner, () => {
        const binding = attentionDigest([path, id]);
        const old = this.store.db
          .prepare(
            "SELECT binding_hash,admitted,payload_json FROM attention_subjects WHERE scope_id=? AND kind='loop' AND subject_id=?"
          )
          .get(scopeId, id) as
          | { binding_hash: string; admitted: number; payload_json: string }
          | undefined;
        if (old && old.binding_hash !== binding)
          throw new Error("loop_binding_conflict");
        const count = (
          this.store.db
            .prepare(
              "SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM attention_subjects WHERE admitted=1"
            )
            .get() as { n: number }
        ).n;
        const released =
          old && JSON.parse(old.payload_json).reason === "owner_released";
        const admitted = released
          ? 0
          : old?.admitted === 1 || count < 100
          ? 1
          : 0;
        this.store.db
          .prepare(
            `INSERT INTO attention_subjects(scope_id,kind,subject_id,binding_hash,source_version,admitted,discovered_at,sampled_at,generation,payload_json) VALUES(?,'loop',?,?,0,?,?,?,?,?) ON CONFLICT(scope_id,kind,subject_id) DO UPDATE SET admitted=excluded.admitted,payload_json=excluded.payload_json,sampled_at=excluded.sampled_at,generation=excluded.generation`
          )
          .run(
            scopeId,
            id,
            binding,
            admitted,
            Date.now(),
            Date.now(),
            this.observer.owner.generation,
            JSON.stringify({
              ...facts,
              state_path: path,
              reason: released
                ? "owner_released"
                : !admitted
                ? "capacity_exceeded"
                : facts.state === "source_missing"
                ? "source_missing"
                : facts.recovery_required
                ? "recovery_required"
                : "snapshot_only",
            })
          );
      });
      return facts;
    }
    if (method === "attention.ci") {
      const { AttentionCiReader } = await import("@osstack/github");
      this.ciReader ??= new AttentionCiReader();
      return await this.ciReader.read({
        repo: str("github_repo"),
        pr: p["pr"],
        head: str("head"),
        required: p["required"],
      });
    }
    if (method === "attention.brief")
      return this.experience.brief(scopeId, str("actor"));
    if (method === "attention.posture") {
      const posture = str("posture");
      if (!["quiet", "away", "attended"].includes(posture))
        throw new Error("invalid_posture");
      this.experience.posture(
        scopeId,
        str("actor"),
        posture as "quiet" | "away" | "attended"
      );
      return { posture };
    }
    if (method === "attention.seen") {
      if (!Array.isArray(p["revisions"])) throw new Error("invalid_revisions");
      this.experience.seen(scopeId, str("actor"), p["revisions"]);
      return { marked: true };
    }
    if (method === "attention.foreground") {
      if (!Array.isArray(p["subjects"])) throw new Error("invalid_subjects");
      return this.experience.foreground(scopeId, p["subjects"]);
    }
    if (method === "attention.handoff")
      return this.experience.handoff(scopeId, str("client_id"), str("token"));
    if (method === "attention.turn-end")
      return this.experience.turnEnd(
        scopeId,
        str("client_id"),
        str("token"),
        str("role")
      );
    if (method === "attention.deliveries")
      return this.store.db
        .prepare(
          "SELECT * FROM attention_deliveries WHERE scope_id=? AND delivery_id>? ORDER BY delivery_id LIMIT 100"
        )
        .all(scopeId, typeof p["after"] === "string" ? p["after"] : "");
    if (method === "attention.inventory")
      return this.observer.inventory(
        scopeId,
        Date.now(),
        typeof p["after"] === "string" ? p["after"] : ""
      );
    if (method === "attention.inbox") {
      const items = this.store.listItems(
        scopeId,
        typeof p["after"] === "string" ? p["after"] : ""
      );
      const counts = this.store.db
        .prepare(
          "SELECT state,COUNT(*) total FROM attention_items WHERE scope_id=? GROUP BY state"
        )
        .all(scopeId);
      return AttentionInbox.parse({
        schema_version: 1,
        execution_takeover: false,
        scope_id: scopeId,
        items,
        counts,
        next_after: items.length === 100 ? items.at(-1)!.item_id : null,
        coverage: this.observer.inventory(scopeId).coverage,
      });
    }
    if (method === "attention.get") {
      const item = this.store.getItem(scopeId, str("item_id"));
      if (!item) throw new Error("not_found");
      return item;
    }
    throw new Error("unsupported_attention_method");
  }
}


FINAL SOURCE packages/core/src/state/attention-actions.ts
import { realpathSync } from "node:fs";
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import type Database from "better-sqlite3";
import { SessionDecision } from "@osstack/protocol";
import { withWrite } from "./db.js";
import { SessionRepository, type SessionMessageInput } from "./sessions.js";
import {
  AttentionStore,
  attentionDigest,
  type AttentionOwner,
} from "./attention-store.js";
/** Common human reservation, serialized with collaborator writes in the same DB. */
export function reserveSessionInstruction(
  db: Database.Database,
  path: string,
  message: SessionMessageInput,
  version: number,
  now: number,
  takeover = false,
  takeoverGrantId?: string
) {
  return withWrite(db, path, () => {
    const sessions = new SessionRepository(db, path);
    if (sessions.getMessage(message.message_id))
      return sessions.applyInbound(message, version, now);
    if (
      db
        .prepare(
          "SELECT 1 FROM session_messages WHERE session_id=? AND direction='inbound' AND state IN ('accepted','failed') AND message_id NOT IN (SELECT message_id FROM attention_receipt_resolutions) LIMIT 1"
        )
        .get(message.session_id)
    )
      throw new Error("delivery_uncertain");
    if (
      db
        .prepare(
          "SELECT 1 FROM session_continuation_attempts a JOIN session_continuation_grants g ON a.grant_id=g.grant_id WHERE g.session_id=? AND a.status IN ('checking','reserved') LIMIT 1"
        )
        .get(message.session_id)
    )
      throw new Error("continuation_in_flight");
    const grant = db
      .prepare(
        "SELECT grant_id FROM session_continuation_grants WHERE session_id=? AND status='active'"
      )
      .get(message.session_id) as { grant_id: string } | undefined;
    if (grant && takeover && takeoverGrantId !== grant.grant_id)
      throw new Error("takeover_grant_changed");
    if (grant && !takeover) throw new Error("grant_owned");
    const snapshot = sessions.latestSnapshot(message.session_id);
    if (snapshot.state === "awaiting_decision" && snapshot.decision === null)
      throw new Error("invalid_decision");
    const decision = SessionDecision.safeParse(snapshot.decision);
    const raw = snapshot.decision as Record<string, unknown> | null;
    const legacy =
      raw !== null &&
      typeof raw === "object" &&
      !Array.isArray(raw) &&
      Object.keys(raw).every((k) => ["question", "choices"].includes(k)) &&
      Array.isArray(raw["choices"]) &&
      raw["choices"].every((v) => typeof v === "string") &&
      (raw["question"] === undefined || typeof raw["question"] === "string");
    if (snapshot.decision !== null && !decision.success && !legacy)
      throw new Error("invalid_decision");

    if (
      decision.success &&
      ["risk_approval", "task_authorization"].includes(
        decision.data.continuation?.gate_kind ?? ""
      )
    )
      throw new Error("signed_approval_required");
    if (grant)
      db.prepare(
        "UPDATE session_continuation_grants SET status='blocked',reason='human_takeover',updated_at=? WHERE grant_id=?"
      ).run(now, grant.grant_id);
    return sessions.applyInbound(message, version, now);
  });
}
export interface AttentionAnswer {
  scope_id: string;
  item_id: string;
  action_id: string;
  revision: number;
  content_hash: string;
  text: string;
  takeover: boolean;
  takeover_grant_id?: string;
}
interface Action {
  action_id: string;
  scope_id: string;
  item_id: string;
  payload_hash: string;
  message_id: string;
  state: string;
  created_at: number;
  updated_at: number;
}
export class AttentionActions {
  constructor(readonly store: AttentionStore, readonly owner: AttentionOwner) {}
  reserve(input: AttentionAnswer, now = Date.now()) {
    if (
      !input.action_id ||
      input.action_id.length > 256 ||
      !input.text.trim() ||
      input.text.length > 16000
    )
      throw new Error("invalid_answer");
    return this.store.write(this.owner, () => {
      const db = this.store.db;
      const digest = attentionDigest(input);
      const prior = db
        .prepare("SELECT * FROM attention_actions WHERE action_id=?")
        .get(input.action_id) as Action | undefined;
      if (prior) {
        if (prior.payload_hash !== digest)
          throw new Error("action_id_conflict");
        return {
          ...this.reconcile(prior, now),
          replay: true,
          reservation_version: 0,
        };
      }
      const item = this.store.getItem(input.scope_id, input.item_id);
      const scope = this.store.getScope(input.scope_id);
      if (!item || !scope) throw new Error("not_found");
      if (
        item.state !== "open" ||
        item.revision !== input.revision ||
        item.content_hash !== input.content_hash
      )
        throw new Error("stale_item");
      if (
        db
          .prepare("SELECT 1 FROM attention_actions WHERE item_id=?")
          .get(item.item_id)
      )
        throw new Error("action_already_reserved");
      const repo = new SessionRepository(db, this.store.path);
      const source = repo.getSession(item.session_id);
      const latest = repo.latestSnapshot(item.session_id);
      if (source && source.state !== "awaiting_decision")
        throw new Error("session_not_awaiting_decision");
      if (
        !source ||
        source.task_id !== scope.task_id ||
        realpathSync(source.repo_path) !== scope.repo_path ||
        latest.turn_id !== item.turn_id ||
        source.version !== item.source_version ||
        attentionDigest(SessionDecision.parse(latest.decision)) !==
          item.content_hash
      )
        throw new Error("source_changed");
      if (
        !db
          .prepare(
            "SELECT 1 FROM tasks t JOIN runs r ON r.task_id=t.task_id WHERE t.task_id=? AND r.run_id=? AND t.tenant_id IS NULL AND r.tenant_id IS NULL"
          )
          .get(scope.task_id, source.run_id)
      )
        throw new Error("source_changed");
      const messageId = `attention-${randomUUID()}`;
      const reservation = reserveSessionInstruction(
        db,
        this.store.path,
        {
          message_id: messageId,
          session_id: item.session_id,
          turn_id: item.turn_id,
          direction: "inbound",
          kind: "instruction.send",
          payload: { text: input.text },
          state: "accepted",
          created_at: now / 1000,
        },
        source.version,
        now / 1000,
        input.takeover,
        input.takeover_grant_id
      );
      db.prepare(
        "INSERT INTO attention_actions VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,'reserved',?,?)"
      ).run(
        input.action_id,
        input.scope_id,
        input.item_id,
        digest,
        messageId,
        now,
        now
      );
      db.prepare(
        "UPDATE attention_items SET state='answer_reserved',revision=revision+1,updated_at=? WHERE item_id=?"
      ).run(now, item.item_id);
      return {
        action_id: input.action_id,
        scope_id: input.scope_id,
        item_id: input.item_id,
        payload_hash: digest,
        message_id: messageId,
        state: "reserved",
        created_at: now,
        updated_at: now,
        replay: false,
        reservation_version: reservation.session_version,
      };
    });
  }
  replay(input: AttentionAnswer, applyingMessageId?: string) {
    const prior = this.store.db
      .prepare("SELECT * FROM attention_actions WHERE action_id=?")
      .get(input.action_id) as Action | undefined;
    if (!prior) return undefined;
    if (prior.payload_hash !== attentionDigest(input))
      throw new Error("action_id_conflict");
    if (prior.message_id === applyingMessageId)
      return { ...prior, state: "in_flight", replay: true };
    return { ...this.reconcile(prior), replay: true };
  }
  abandon(
    input: {
      scope_id: string;
      session_id: string;
      message_id: string;
      expected_version: number;
      reason: string;
      evidence_sha256: string;
    },
    now = Date.now()
  ) {
    if (
      !input.reason ||
      input.reason.length > 1000 ||
      !/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(input.evidence_sha256)
    )
      throw new Error("resolution_evidence_required");
    return this.store.write(this.owner, () => {
      const db = this.store.db;
      const session = new SessionRepository(db, this.store.path).getSession(
        input.session_id
      );
      if (!session || session.version !== input.expected_version)
        throw new Error("version_conflict");
      const scope = this.store.getScope(input.scope_id);
      if (
        !scope ||
        scope.task_id !== session.task_id ||
        scope.repo_path !== realpathSync(session.repo_path)
      )
        throw new Error("scope_mismatch");
      const message = db
        .prepare(
          "SELECT state FROM session_messages WHERE message_id=? AND session_id=? AND direction='inbound'"
        )
        .get(input.message_id, input.session_id) as
        | { state: string }
        | undefined;
      if (!message || !["accepted", "failed"].includes(message.state))
        throw new Error("receipt_not_uncertain");
      if (
        db
          .prepare(
            "SELECT 1 FROM session_continuation_attempts a JOIN session_continuation_grants g ON a.grant_id=g.grant_id WHERE g.session_id=? AND a.status IN ('checking','reserved')"
          )
          .get(input.session_id)
      )
        throw new Error("continuation_in_flight");
      const prior = db
        .prepare(
          "SELECT * FROM attention_receipt_resolutions WHERE message_id=?"
        )
        .get(input.message_id) as
        | { reason: string; evidence_sha256: string }
        | undefined;
      if (
        prior &&
        (prior.reason !== input.reason ||
          prior.evidence_sha256 !== input.evidence_sha256)
      )
        throw new Error("resolution_conflict");
      db.prepare(
        "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO attention_receipt_resolutions VALUES(?,?,?,'abandoned_unknown',?,?,?)"
      ).run(
        input.message_id,
        input.session_id,
        input.expected_version,
        input.reason,
        input.evidence_sha256,
        now
      );
      db.prepare(
        "UPDATE attention_actions SET state='abandoned_unknown',updated_at=? WHERE message_id=?"
      ).run(now, input.message_id);
      db.prepare(
        "UPDATE attention_items SET state='answer_abandoned',revision=revision+1,updated_at=? WHERE item_id IN (SELECT item_id FROM attention_actions WHERE message_id=?) AND state<>'answer_abandoned'"
      ).run(now, input.message_id);
      return {
        disposition: "abandoned_unknown",
        message_id: input.message_id,
        replayed: false,
        execution_outcome: "unknown",
        next_action:
          "New manual instruction only; original message is never resent",
      };
    });
  }
  recover(): void {
    const rows = this.store.db
      .prepare(
        "SELECT * FROM attention_actions WHERE state IN ('reserved','uncertain')"
      )
      .all() as Action[];
    for (const row of rows) this.reconcile(row);
  }
  reconcile(action: Action, now = Date.now()): Action {
    return this.store.write(this.owner, () => {
      if (
        this.store.db
          .prepare(
            "SELECT 1 FROM attention_receipt_resolutions WHERE message_id=?"
          )
          .get(action.message_id)
      )
        return { ...action, state: "abandoned_unknown" };
      const receipt = this.store.db
        .prepare("SELECT state FROM session_messages WHERE message_id=?")
        .get(action.message_id) as { state: string } | undefined;
      const state =
        receipt?.state === "applied"
          ? "applied"
          : receipt?.state === "failed"
          ? "uncertain"
          : "uncertain";
      this.store.db
        .prepare(
          "UPDATE attention_actions SET state=?,updated_at=? WHERE action_id=?"
        )
        .run(state, now, action.action_id);
      this.store.db
        .prepare(
          "UPDATE attention_items SET state=?,revision=revision+1,updated_at=? WHERE item_id=? AND state<>?"
        )
        .run(
          state === "applied" ? "answer_applied" : "answer_uncertain",
          now,
          action.item_id,
          state === "applied" ? "answer_applied" : "answer_uncertain"
        );
      return { ...action, state, updated_at: now };
    });
  }
}


FINAL SOURCE packages/core/src/state/attention-store.ts
import { createHash, randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import { realpathSync, statSync } from "node:fs";
import type Database from "better-sqlite3";
import { SessionDecision } from "@osstack/protocol";
import { withWrite } from "./db.js";

export function attentionDigest(value: unknown): string {
  const canonical = (v: unknown): string => {
    if (Array.isArray(v)) return `[${v.map(canonical).join(",")}]`;
    if (v !== null && typeof v === "object")
      return `{${Object.entries(v)
        .sort(([a], [b]) => (a < b ? -1 : a > b ? 1 : 0))
        .map(([k, x]) => `${JSON.stringify(k)}:${canonical(x)}`)
        .join(",")}}`;
    return JSON.stringify(v);
  };
  return createHash("sha256").update(canonical(value)).digest("hex");
}
export interface AttentionOwner {
  instance_id: string;
  generation: number;
  home_id: string;
}
export interface AttentionScope {
  scope_id: string;
  task_id: string;
  repo_path: string;
  discovery_after: string;
  scan_complete: number;
  sampled_at: number;
  deadline: number;
  generation: number;
  error: string | null;
}
export interface AttentionItem {
  item_id: string;
  scope_id: string;
  session_id: string;
  turn_id: string;
  decision_id: string;
  content_hash: string;
  decision: SessionDecision;
  source_version: number;
  revision: number;
  state: string;
  created_at: number;
  updated_at: number;
  external_message_id: string | null;
}
type ItemRow = Omit<AttentionItem, "decision"> & { decision_json: string };
function parseItem(row: ItemRow): AttentionItem {
  const { decision_json, ...rest } = row;
  return {
    ...rest,
    decision: SessionDecision.parse(JSON.parse(decision_json)),
  };
}
export class AttentionStore {
  constructor(readonly db: Database.Database, readonly path: string) {}
  /** Only called AFTER the daemon's process lock is acquired. Not an executor lease. */
  claimOwner(instanceId: string): AttentionOwner {
    return withWrite(this.db, this.path, () => {
      this.db
        .prepare(
          `INSERT INTO daemon_owner(singleton,home_id,instance_id,generation) VALUES(1,?,?,1)
        ON CONFLICT(singleton) DO UPDATE SET instance_id=excluded.instance_id,generation=generation+1`
        )
        .run(randomUUID(), instanceId);
      return this.db
        .prepare(
          "SELECT home_id,instance_id,generation FROM daemon_owner WHERE singleton=1"
        )
        .get() as AttentionOwner;
    });
  }
  assertOwner(owner: AttentionOwner): void {
    if (
      !this.db
        .prepare(
          "SELECT 1 FROM daemon_owner WHERE singleton=1 AND home_id=? AND instance_id=? AND generation=?"
        )
        .get(owner.home_id, owner.instance_id, owner.generation)
    )
      throw new Error("stale_owner");
  }
  write<T>(owner: AttentionOwner, fn: () => T): T {
    if (this.db.inTransaction) {
      this.assertOwner(owner);
      return fn();
    }
    return withWrite(this.db, this.path, () => {
      this.assertOwner(owner);
      return fn();
    });
  }
  registerScope(
    owner: AttentionOwner,
    taskId: string,
    repoPath: string
  ): AttentionScope {
    const canonicalRepo = realpathSync(repoPath);
    if (!statSync(canonicalRepo).isDirectory())
      throw new Error("repository_directory_required");
    return this.write(owner, () => {
      const task = this.db
        .prepare(
          "SELECT created_at FROM tasks WHERE task_id=? AND tenant_id IS NULL"
        )
        .get(taskId) as { created_at: number } | undefined;
      if (!task) throw new Error("not_found");
      if (
        (
          this.db.prepare("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM attention_scopes").get() as {
            n: number;
          }
        ).n >= 100 &&
        !this.db
          .prepare(
            "SELECT 1 FROM attention_scopes WHERE task_id=? AND repo_path=?"
          )
          .get(taskId, canonicalRepo)
      )
        throw new Error("scope_capacity");
      const scopeId = attentionDigest([
        owner.home_id,
        taskId,
        canonicalRepo,
        task.created_at,
      ]);
      this.db
        .prepare(
          `INSERT INTO attention_scopes(scope_id,task_id,repo_path,generation) VALUES(?,?,?,?)
        ON CONFLICT(scope_id) DO NOTHING`
        )
        .run(scopeId, taskId, canonicalRepo, owner.generation);
      return this.getScope(scopeId)!;
    });
  }
  getScope(scopeId: string): AttentionScope | undefined {
    return this.db
      .prepare("SELECT * FROM attention_scopes WHERE scope_id=?")
      .get(scopeId) as AttentionScope | undefined;
  }
  listScopes(): AttentionScope[] {
    return this.db
      .prepare("SELECT * FROM attention_scopes ORDER BY scope_id")
      .all() as AttentionScope[];
  }
  getItem(scopeId: string, itemId: string): AttentionItem | undefined {
    const row = this.db
      .prepare("SELECT * FROM attention_items WHERE scope_id=? AND item_id=?")
      .get(scopeId, itemId) as ItemRow | undefined;
    return row ? parseItem(row) : undefined;
  }
  listItems(scopeId: string, after = "", limit = 100): AttentionItem[] {
    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(limit) || limit < 1 || limit > 100)
      throw new Error("invalid_limit");
    return (
      this.db
        .prepare(
          "SELECT * FROM attention_items WHERE scope_id=? AND item_id>? ORDER BY item_id LIMIT ?"
        )
        .all(scopeId, after, limit) as ItemRow[]
    ).map(parseItem);
  }
  projectDecision(
    owner: AttentionOwner,
    input: {
      scopeId: string;
      sessionId: string;
      turnId: string;
      sourceVersion: number;
      decision: unknown;
      now: number;
    }
  ): AttentionItem {
    const decision = SessionDecision.parse(input.decision);
    const contentHash = attentionDigest(decision);
    const itemId = attentionDigest([
      input.scopeId,
      input.sessionId,
      input.turnId,
      decision.decision_id,
    ]);
    if (
      !Number.isSafeInteger(input.sourceVersion) ||
      input.sourceVersion < 1 ||
      !Number.isFinite(input.now)
    )
      throw new Error("invalid_source_version");
    return this.write(owner, () => {
      if (!this.getScope(input.scopeId)) throw new Error("not_found");
      const existing = this.getItem(input.scopeId, itemId);
      if (existing && existing.content_hash !== contentHash)
        throw new Error("decision_content_conflict");
      if (existing && existing.source_version > input.sourceVersion)
        throw new Error("stale_source");
      this.db
        .prepare(
          `INSERT INTO attention_items(item_id,scope_id,session_id,turn_id,decision_id,content_hash,decision_json,source_version,created_at,updated_at)
        VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?) ON CONFLICT(item_id) DO UPDATE SET
        revision=revision+CASE WHEN source_version<>excluded.source_version OR state='source_missing' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END,
        state=CASE WHEN state='source_missing' THEN 'open' ELSE state END,
        source_version=excluded.source_version,updated_at=excluded.updated_at`
        )
        .run(
          itemId,
          input.scopeId,
          input.sessionId,
          input.turnId,
          decision.decision_id,
          contentHash,
          JSON.stringify(decision),
          input.sourceVersion,
          input.now,
          input.now
        );
      return this.getItem(input.scopeId, itemId)!;
    });
  }
  release(
    owner: AttentionOwner,
    scopeId: string,
    kind: string,
    subjectId: string,
    expectedVersion: number
  ): void {
    if (!["session", "run", "loop"].includes(kind))
      throw new Error("invalid_subject_kind");
    this.write(owner, () => {
      const row = this.db
        .prepare(
          "SELECT payload_json,source_version FROM attention_subjects WHERE scope_id=? AND kind=? AND subject_id=?"
        )
        .get(scopeId, kind, subjectId) as
        | { payload_json: string; source_version: number }
        | undefined;
      if (!row || row.source_version !== expectedVersion)
        throw new Error("subject_version_conflict");
      this.db
        .prepare(
          "UPDATE attention_subjects SET admitted=0,payload_json=? WHERE scope_id=? AND kind=? AND subject_id=?"
        )
        .run(
          JSON.stringify({
            ...JSON.parse(row.payload_json),
            reason: "owner_released",
          }),
          scopeId,
          kind,
          subjectId
        );
    });
  }
  markMissing(owner: AttentionOwner, scopeId: string, sessionId: string): void {
    this.write(owner, () =>
      this.db
        .prepare(
          `UPDATE attention_items SET state='source_missing',revision=revision+1
      WHERE scope_id=? AND session_id=? AND state='open'`
        )
        .run(scopeId, sessionId)
    );
  }
}


FINAL SOURCE packages/core/src/state/attention-observer.ts
import { classifyAttentionWait, type WaitInput } from "../attention.js";
import { realpathSync } from "node:fs";
import {
  attentionDigest,
  AttentionStore,
  type AttentionOwner,
  type AttentionScope,
} from "./attention-store.js";
interface Subject {
  scope_id: string;
  kind: string;
  subject_id: string;
  binding_hash: string;
  source_version: number;
  ordinal_cursor: number;
  admitted: number;
  discovered_at: number;
  sampled_at: number;
  generation: number;
  payload_json: string;
}
interface Source {
  session_id: string;
  run_id: string;
  request_sha256: string;
  agent_id: string;
  native_session_id: string | null;
  repo_path: string;
  version: number;
  state: string;
}
interface Turn {
  turn_id: string;
  ordinal: number;
  decision_json: string | null;
  state: string;
}
